## How to read this file

This file is **append-only and long, and most of it is history.** You do not need all of it to write.

- **For a cold start, read four things, in order:** ***Who is where at end of chapter 298*** (the first block
  below this list, written at the Volume 5 Batch 0004 close and the live position for the whole run), ***What
  Volume 3 must not do*** (further down), which Volume 4 inherited in full and which Volume 5 inherited through
  it, ***Who is where at end of chapter 238*** (below that one), which is where Volume 5 started, and ***Who is
  where at end of chapter 252*** (the Volume 5 Batch 0001 record, kept because Volume 5's own cold-reader block
  sits above it). **Then read `## The Volume 5 hand-off` further down this file, which is the block Volume 5's
  planner opened on, and `## The protagonist, answered in writing` immediately above it, which is a standing
  requirement on Volume 5 and not a note.** The Volume 5 close phase should not have to read past those.
- **`outline/volume-05.md` is the authority for Volume 5 and `outline/batches/volume-05-batch-0001.md` is the
  card file Volume 5 Batch 0001 was drafted against.** Every card beat in that file is on the page and the
  file is not to be re-planned. **Batch 0002 (ch 253–267) was drafted against
  `workspace/volume-05-batch-0002/PROMPT.md` with no card file, and every pinned item in that prompt is on the
  page. Batch 0003 (ch 268–283) has no card file; its drafting prompt was
  `workspace/volume-05-batch-0003/PROMPT.md`. Batch 0004 (ch 284–298), the last batch of the volume, has no card
  file either; its drafting prompt was `workspace/volume-05-batch-0004/PROMPT.md`, and every pinned item in it
  is on the page.**
- **`## Volume 5 close audit — CLOSED`** is the record of the Volume 5 close. **Read it before planning Volume 6** — it carries the fifteen checks, twelve repairs made in the chapters (including seven instances of authorial scaffolding and the only calendar month in the volume), nine findings recorded and not repaired, the fifteen preserved continuity facts walked one at a time, and **the protagonist answered in writing, which is the standing requirement `outline/series.md` §2 puts on every volume from here.**

  planning Volume 5, or using any figure from this run's last volume** — it carries the four repairs made in

## Who is where at end of chapter 252 — VOLUME 5 BATCH 0001 DRAFTED (cold reader starts here)

Full record: `state/continuity.md` under ***Established facts, Volume 5 Batch 0001 (ch 239–252) — KEEPING AND BEING KEPT*** and ***Volume 5 running ledger — at end of ch 252***. **Day 506 — the thirteenth of a month. She is past the last working point of a line of work, in somebody's ordinary country, at a stone yard, and she has been booked to the end of the season.**

- **Ning Yan** — **FAR SIDE, past the last working point, at a stone yard and a trestle about six hundred yards past the working ground.** Foundation 9, unchanged for fourteen chapters, **and there is nothing above nine anywhere in the world.** Ceiling on the front of the relicensed licence is still **Foundation the first stage** and **she has still never opened it**; seven conditions referenced and never re-listed; the claim the seventh and last thing on the paper.
- **Body at the last page.** Short breath **four counts everywhere.** **THE RIGHT INDEX FINGER IS GONE, PERMANENTLY** — **four fingers' worth of grip left on that hand**, and it was the hand the load came down across at ch 242. The order gone out of her left hand with the heel of it; eleven holds out of about seventy tried. Every county hour about eleven minutes with the flat of a second finger. Right arm open a third of the time. Aperture cracked through and permanent. **Not hungry since the first Foundation.** Cold the way a stone is cold. Bread from a far-side store at five coppers a day. **Eighteen years old.**
- **What the fourteen chapters did.** **She worked the far reach and the book was opened once** (239). **The country asked nothing and her strength had no price on it, and she got one true answer about the reach from a man with no reason to give it** (240). **The pay-cycle was established — the seventh, the seventeenth, the twenty-seventh — and no day can be moved by anybody** (241). **She took the heavy end and a load came down across the hand with four fingers on it, and there was nobody within nine days of road to tell** (242). **Wei Ran gave a rate and a road, and the road west costs ninety coppers when it opens, and it is shut for the season** (243). **A stranger at a pot gave her three facts about nine days west and she could not ask a second question** (244). **She asked for a season out loud at a trestle on a pay-day** (245). **She worked out that her column is the only instrument on this side of the line and did not stop working** (246). **IRREVERSIBLE: she said her name the second time on that ground and is booked to the end of the season, and cannot be un-entered** (247). **The Hunter said he does not want the claim answered either** (248). **Nine stubs in four marks, laid out clean, and for the first time she can prove she was somewhere** (249). **The fire was free and was not warm, and it was let down without asking her** (250). **The office said out loud, in front of six men, that the claim is actionable on their side** (251). **The price is her column, and she said no once, in her own head, with nobody hearing** (252).
- **The one thing the batch changed.** She is not a woman passing through a place any more. **She is a column of hours in a book that an office four hundred yards away has just been told it can be paid against, and she is the only person in the world who can give that column up, and she has said no once.**
- **The claim.** **Eight hundred jin, standing, entered in good order, travelling on the licence, not affected by the relicensing, and ACTIONABLE ON THE FAR SIDE** — confirmed in the ordinary voice in a room with a stove in it, in front of six men, **by a man who does not know the figure is hers and has never asked.** The price of acting is her own hours. **Not answered, not paid, not assessed, not torn off.** Hou Sanliang unpaid, patient and never met. The date in Lowwater untouched. Luo Ping a direction. **No draft has been written and none has been sent — that is Batch 0002's.**
- **Yuan.** Voice. **Six sentences across fourteen chapters, at most two in one chapter, all present tense, and none of them bought anything.** **No figure of what is left of him is on any page in ch 239–252; the *about six weeks* said once at ch 236 is the last figure of him in the manuscript.** Merit settled **zero** times; nothing killed; nothing cleared; the channel not restored. **Ledger is hers to order and she has not ordered it and has not thought about ordering it.** **The sustained scene in which he is on the page in his own right is planned at ch 272, *The Man the Board Keeps*, in Batch 0003, and nothing in this batch touches it.**
- **Bai Zhu.** **At nine feet at ch 248, in the ordinary voice, with the notebook shut: he does not want the claim answered by the far side either, because an answered claim is an answer somebody above him has to reckon with and he has never believed the reckoning would be the right one — and the same is true of his own order and he has said that before.** The standing one-breath form holds: bought nothing, threatened nothing, touched nothing, entered nothing, asked nothing. **He certified nothing, bought nothing and is not a villain.** He has said he will be on that side of the post for the rest of the run and will not come inside nine feet again unless she puts him there. **His request is still pending and the line's own people still do not know she exists.**
- **Inside the bound, by report only, at ch 244.** **A tower with a light in it that is on at the fourth hour and does not stop. A bundle going down a stair and a clerk's arms going up four flights with it and coming down empty. A floor of nine men and the same nine and all of them still on it.** From a hauler at a pot who has never met Feng Lushi and does not know his name. **Feng does not appear. No scene west of the line. No document arrives. His fair copy of the Foundation column past the line is ch 262 and is unspent.**
- **The far side is ordinary country.** A yard, a trestle, a fire, eleven men and a man at the ramp called Da; a store with a counter and five coppers; a low building with a counter, a stove and six men; a post with a board on it that a keeper of about fifty-five fills off a cart every nine or ten days; a mill two miles east with a wheel that is not turning. **It asked her nothing.** Not one man said her name, and the strongest body within nine days of road is measured on it entirely by what she turns over with a bar.
- **Pay and stubs.** **Pays fall on the seventh, the seventeenth and the twenty-seventh; a day not worked is a day not paid and no day can be moved in either direction by anybody.** Five in, five out, and the number in her hand the same at the start and the end of every day. **Nine counterfoils in four marks in the oilcloth above the Clay Plate, none checkable by anybody within nine days of road, and the dates come out clean.**
- **The register still reads *reconciled*. The Clay Plate is still worn and was not described. MEMBER ABSENT stands three times and was not raised. Shen Xiu unforgiven and unthanked. The three sponsors silent. Ning Wu does not appear. What became of the eleven is not raised. The ch 150 refusal is not softened, deferred, reopened or regretted in any line. The year of non-forgiveness was not mentioned at all in these fourteen chapters and is still carrying.** She does not forgive him and she does not tell him to stop.
- **The road that goes back inside is shut for the season** and gets its opening day in Batch 0002. Wei Ran quoted **ninety coppers** for a person and one bag when it opens; the coming is a different number and nobody has quoted it.
- **The surface answer is NOT paid.** Batch 0001 pays the narrower thing only: *a figure is only paid against something, and the only thing in this country that can be charged for is a day somebody worked.* **The volume's surface answer is ch 267, in a worker's mouth, in short sentences, and Batch 0002 must pay it at the last chapter and not before.**
- **The far-side board is not re-counted.** Its image was ch 236 and ch 238. What is on the page now is the routine: a strip goes up every nine or ten days whether or not there is anything in it.

## Who is where at end of chapter 298 — VOLUME 5 CLOSED AND AUDITED (cold reader starts here)

**Day 620 — the seventh of a month. She is past the last working point, at the end of a season that closed on the thirtieth, Golden Core 3, eighteen stubs, no wages and no season.** Full record in `state/continuity.md` (*Established facts, Volume 5 Batch 0004 (ch 284–298) — ASH AND LEAVING* + running ledger at ch 298). *(Corrected at review: the first record of this block gave day 622 and the ninth of a month. Ch 298 is wholly on the seventh — "The seventh of the month came clear", and ch 297 ends "The seventh of the month was tomorrow" — and under the month rule at line 68 the seventh is **day 620**, which is what the In-world date block in this file already said. The batch therefore ends on day 620, not 622.)*

- **Ning Yan — GOLDEN CORE 3**, taken day 600 (the seventeenth, a pay-day) at her own order, in daylight, in the middle of a reach where eleven men were two hundred yards behind her. **The volume's third and last bracket.** Cost: **she is entered as good in a book the bound cannot read, and she cannot be un-entered** — the first cost in five volumes that is not on her body. Paper still Foundation the first stage, never opened.
- **Body:** short breath four counts; **right index gone, four fingers' grip**; **right arm open half the time, permanent, since a face let go on her in a cut at the end of a reach**; nine of eleven holds; turn gone; middle-bank method; aperture cracked permanent; not hungry; cold like stone; **eighteen.**
- **What the batch changed:** the furnace of her own finished and paid for in **two days out of her own column**; the far date read at last — the thirtieth — and **the three working days before it that nobody pays for**; the reach's last run taken off outside the book and outside the wage; **the heavy end and the ramp closed for good**; **Golden Core 3 and the entry as good**; **the claim ANSWERED** and the Lowwater date **torn off by Luo Ping's hand**; **Hou paid and never met**; **Ning Bo's account discharged where it can be closed**; **Ning Wu's decade entered as a line with no mark on it**; the season closed and the next pay-day producing nothing.
- **The claim: ANSWERED.** Eight hundred jin, answered in ink at ch 294, read out once in a room with nine men in it, drafted west on the carrier's round with no date, paid into Hou Sanliang's counting room. **The seventh condition discharged and the seven conditions never re-listed. The date came off the page — not struck out, off — by the hand that wrote it. Hou Sanliang is paid and has never been met. Luo Ping was never met.**
- **Ning Bo's account.** Discharged in the far-side office's own book: *balance carried forward, Ning Yan, of Lowwater, against the furnace account of Ning Bo, discharged; six years carried; no balance standing; nothing carried against the name.* **It does nothing in Lowwater, because no instrument in nine days of road reaches a county, and the account is closed where it can be closed.** The county's furnace column still reads unsettled and the register still reads *reconciled*.
- **Ning Wu.** A line in the same book: name, county, ten years, ten years of his own, no mark on it, not read out. **He does not appear and is not met. The list he is on is nine days of road away, or further, in the hands of somebody whose name the woman who told her did not have; the line about him is four hundred yards behind her.**
- **Yuan.** **Merit settled a second time (ch 290) on a listed run closed, not on a death; told before spent; the share held six days and spent at ch 296 out of her foundation, and the price is the one nobody can name or measure.** **Ledger ended as a standing held stage at ch 272 and what replaces it is his readability, said on the page.** No figure of what is left of him anywhere in ch 284–298.
- **The Hunter.** One card, ch 297, nine feet off a track, one true sentence, no question, no name on anything he has written. **His request is still pending.** The line's own people still do not know she exists.
- **Untouched:** the relicensed licence never opened; the register read once and never corrected; the Clay Plate worn against her ribs; **MEMBER ABSENT ×3, unraised**; the year of non-forgiveness carrying and untouched; the three sponsors silent; **Feng never on the page**, no second Lung, no second appointment; nothing above the Standing Office of the Second Account named; *kiln-born* zero; what became of the eleven not raised; the ch 150 refusal not softened. **The surface answer paid once at ch 267 and never re-voiced. The tenth part is not given.**
- **The volume is CLOSED AND AUDITED.** Full table, every repair and the protagonist answer are in **`## Volume 5 close audit — CLOSED`** below. **`outline/volume-06.md` does not exist yet.** Next phase: **the Volume 6 planning phase**, prompt `workspace/volume-05-close-record/PROMPT.md`.

### The fifteen checks

### Nine findings recorded and not repaired, with the reason

1. **The identical sentence *He said one thing that night and it was present tense and it bought nothing* stands four times verbatim (ch 261, 262, 264, 267) and three more in the *evening* variant (ch 263, 265, 266), and the construction runs the whole of Batch 0002 — eleven chapters, 253–267.** It is zero in ch 268–298. **Recorded, not repaired.** It is a ledger line rather than emphasis: it always precedes his speech, it is never a chapter's closing line, and the utterance under each tag is different every time. It is the batch's one deliberate device for marking the present-tense rule, it was audited with Batch 0002 before Batch 0003 was written, and Batch 0003's review already broke it. Breaking it here would delete an authorised motif on a stylistic preference, not a fault. The audit's measurement is recorded because the run's own note (*seven times in ch 261–267*) is right only if the three *evening* variants are counted as the same construction; the verbatim string is four.
2. **The present-tense block at ch 297 (*What It Is*).** Four paragraphs of close third on Ning Yan in the present tense, in a book that admits one present frame and gives it to Yuan. **Recorded, not repaired.** The construction it uses — *It is a line in a book…* / *That is the whole of what she has done…* — is the volume's and the book's established register for a durable state and has direct precedent in the same volume (ch 239:31, 241:11, 249:23, 251:101, 252:49). No completed event is narrated in the present; the only present-perfect is *what she has done*, which is the flatness the paragraph is for. The rule's purpose — that the present marks his frame — is not in play, because the block is a register, not a viewpoint.
3. **Wei Ran is on the page four times (ch 243, 253, 269, 290), where `outline/volume-05.md` names Batches 0001 and 0004 in two places.** **Recorded, not repaired.** Every appearance is a rate, a road or an opinion in her own register; the operative hard rule — *no harem, nothing in Volume 5 is a romance* — is intact, and ch 290 was rewritten at this close so that the sentence saying so no longer says *nothing in this book*. Cutting two working cards to satisfy a batch-range statement would be a structural change and not a repair.
4. **Bai Zhu is named aloud in the volume only at ch 297**, where Volume 4's close recorded him as *named once at ch 150, spoken twice more at ch 189 and 196*. **Recorded.** The operative prohibition — no document he signs carries his name — holds, and at ch 297 he writes nothing at all. The one card in this volume (297) needed him named because it is the only place in Volume 5 he is on the page in person.
5. **`reconciled` is read on the page seven times across the volume** (250, 279, 282, 292, 294, 296, 298) where required fact 10 says *read once at the close for what it is*. **Recorded, not repaired.** It is read **once as the subject of a chapter** — ch 282, *The Reconciled Page* — and the other six are references in the course of other business, every one of them recording that it stands and none of them correcting it. The rule's purpose is that it is never corrected, and it never is.
6. **The volume never carries the four weeks every foundation costs her as a stated fact.** The outline says Volume 5 *carries them in the body*; what is on the page is the cost of each stage and the unnameable tenth item of the list at ch 291, which is the carrying without the arithmetic. **Recorded, not repaired** — adding the four weeks would be naming the thing `outline/ending.md` §8 reserves for ch 1000.
7. **`about four seconds` still stands twice after repair**, at ch 290 and ch 293. **Recorded deliberately** so the next phase knows the figure was thinned from four to two and why, rather than finding it and finishing the job.
8. **The claim's fourth movement — ch 237, 260, 268 and 294–295 — is four movements, not three.** The audit prompt counts three (237 → 260 → 268 → 294–295 as *moved* and *answered*). **Recorded** so the next planner counts from the page: it was read aloud, moved by draft, authorised onto the far-side book, and answered. Nothing is wrong; the count in the prompt compresses the authorisation into the answer.
9. **`workspace/volume-05-close/` holds no `.done` marker**, as every other finished phase directory does, and `workspace/continuation/` never held one. **Not fixed here: `PHASE_SYSTEM.md` gives the marker to the controller alone.** Recorded for the controller or the operator, on the precedent of the Volume 4 close.

### The four state-file corrections this audit made

- **`state/open-threads.md`** carried the ch 251 interval (*three months ago*) as an unapplied proposed repair from Batch 0002, and this close applied it. Its *Still open* block also carried *Wei Ran appears twice* and the impossible measure. All three corrected.
- **`state/book-metadata.md`** and the Volume line above carried *101,160* as the volume file set. It is **101,204** after this close's repairs, and the per-batch figures are unchanged.
- **`state/chapter-summaries.md`** did not record the volume close at all, and its *Volumes 5–16* block still said the next phase was the Volume 5 close. Corrected, and a **Volume 5 closed — audited** section added beneath it.

### The fifteen preserved items, confirmed one at a time

1. **Golden Core 3, how it was licensed.** Ch 292, day 600, past the line, licensed by no county paper of any kind, paid for out of far-side wages at five coppers a day and materials at far-side prices, entered in a wage-book column that is hers, kept by people who are not a county. 1 at ch 258 day 512, 2 at ch 275 day 556, 3 at ch 292 day 600. **The relicensed licence still says Foundation the first stage on the front of it and she has still never touched it.**
2. **The right index finger.** Lost at the far end of a blind run at about the sixth hour on the twenty-first of a month, in Volume 4, told out loud to a crew. Four fingers' worth of grip through all of Volume 5. **Since ch 288 the right arm is open half the time instead of a third**, which closed the ramp and the heavy end at that yard for good. **Nothing in Volume 5 gives any of it back and nothing offers to** — no offer appears anywhere in the volume.
3. **The relicensing legs in order:** clearance → seal → countersignature, Volume 4, not inverted. **Untouched in Volume 5.** No new clearance, seal or countersignature is invented for Golden Core 1–3, and no chapter says one was required or not required.
4. **What the boundary is, in the terms Volume 4 earned, and whether the tenth part has been given.** A line of work, tended by hand, paid by the day; drift; the residue; the layer logs a thing leaving a place. Ninety parts true, twice said (ch 199, ch 236, both in Volume 4) and **not again in Volume 5**. **The tenth part is not given in Volume 5** and nothing in it proves the crews wrong.
5. **What crossing cost, in hours and in people,** as Volume 4 closed it, plus Volume 5's own: five months of far-side wage-work at five coppers, bread from a store, materials priced for two whole hands, twelve gaps in a true column, and three working days at the end of a season nobody pays for.
6. **The claim: ANSWERED.** Eight hundred jin, the seventh and last condition, read aloud on the far side at ch 237, taken onto the far-side office's own book at ch 268, answered in ink at ch 294, drafted west, date in Hou Sanliang's counting room torn off by the hand that wrote it. **Hou paid, patient to the end, never met.**
7. **The referral and the empty space.** The space holds a date and *Held* (ch 210) and the countersignature on her petition in an office's hand dated from the far side (ch 238). **It is not an answer to the request. The request is still pending at the last page.** A Wall writ is not issued to a field agent. **The line's own people still do not know she exists.**
8. **Feng's Lung, and what the second will cost him — Volume 11, not here.** The first spent (ch 143); the second and any second appointment unspent and unmentioned. What Volume 5 took instead: his stair, his hand, his quarter — small and off page, through the district clerk's arms. What Volume 5 gave a home: **his fair hand copying the Foundation column, copies past the line into a book the far side keeps (ch 262), taken by a keeper's paste without knowing what it was.** Alive, warm, column at five, not thanked, never on the page after that.
9. ***Kiln-born* and the unreadable office.** ***Kiln-born* is still in two heads and on no paper — zero across Volume 5.** The Standing Office of the Second Account was read at ch 236, a middle office with others above it. **Everything above that one office is still unread and stays unread.** No Scribe, no Anchor, nothing about what he was before he died, nothing about her having seen him as a readout.
10. **The register still reads *reconciled*.** Read as the subject of a chapter at ch 282 and never corrected anywhere in the volume.
11. **The plate still worn: STILL WORN**, against her ribs, above eighteen counterfoils in four marks and the dead weight tickets. Described once, at ch 298, as an object she surrendered to nobody, described to nobody and would take off for nobody. **It is the object whose texture she remembers in ch 1000 and Volume 16 owns that.**
12. **Ledger: entered once without her order at ch 272**, at a price stated before the line went in, **and then closed as a standing held stage.** Hers to order no longer; **what replaces it is his readability, and it is said on the page in his own terms** — *I am entered* (272) · *a line on a board… does not need her to look at it* (272) · *I am kept, and I am keeping* (281).
13. **The year of non-forgiveness: STILL RUNNING, and not retired anywhere.** Touched once, at ch 297, at low volume, and it carries: *She did not thank him… and she did not tell him to stop.* She does not forgive him and she does not tell him to stop.
14. **The channel.** About six weeks at the open of Volume 5, restored twice by listed jobs cleared and not by killings (ch 256, ch 290), each share intercepted, told before spent and not coming back. **About weeks at the close, and no figure of it stated anywhere in ch 239–298.**
15. **Wei Ran.** On the page as a line at ch 243, 253, 269 and 290 — a trade, a team, a rate and an opinion, and nothing else. **Nothing in Volume 5 is a romance.**

### The protagonist, answered in writing

**`outline/series.md` §2 names Yuan, male, as the protagonist and `state/book-metadata.md` records
Gender: Male. Volume 5 delivered the correction Volume 4's close promised and it is on the page. The
question this close still has to answer is what the protagonist actually did, and what it cost.**

**What he did across Volume 5, chapter by chapter, and what each of it cost.**

- **ch 256.** He took a share of a settled listed tending and **told her the price before he moved it**, and the price was two of the eleven holds in her left hand. *The share went the way shares go, intercepted and spent, and it did not come back.*
- **ch 258.** He stood in her own order — *You are ordering. And I am here* — and the layer said the one thing it had said since ch 237, and the turn went out of her bar stroke.
- **ch 272.** **He let a keeper's hand enter him as a readable line, away from her hearing, so that her hours would hold past the line without her.** The price was on the table before the line went in: **his unentered state, and the date of the morning he died.** *He keeps the apology. He loses the day.* **This is the first decision in the book that came out of him and not out of her account**, and the chapter says so.
- **ch 281.** He is on the page as a line under a lump of paste, and she keeps four hundred yards from the post on purpose.
- **ch 290.** He took a second share, on the season's last listed run closed, held it six days, **named the price before he moved it** — one line in the record the offices read, and the price out of her foundation, and *not one person alive has ever been able to tell you how much* — and she did not say no.
- **ch 296.** **He spent it, and the family account closed, and it cost her something she cannot name.**

**Every one of those is a decision by the book's protagonist. Four of the six were paid for out of her body
and the last two out of her in ways that leave nothing to point at.**

**What is now true of him that was not true at ch 238.** **He is readable from outside the bound without her,
and therefore findable** — and the chapter that made him findable said on the page, in his own frame, what
that means: *Being read means being findable… an office that looks at a line sends for the line, and being
sent for is a thing that happens to men in rooms with doors.* He is entered in the layer's own reading, on a
strip of cloth kept by people who are not a county, by a man who does not know he has done it. **`outline/ending.md`
§1 requires exactly this before the final volume opens — the Court stage, the Scribe finding him, and the fall.
The prerequisite exists on the page, in his own words, and a man the reader has seen in a room at ch 272 can
carry a fall.** **What Volume 6's planning may not assume: nothing more than this.** He is not stronger, not
restored, not located, not protected, and not forgiven. He has lost a day he will not get back. He has no
hands, no road, and no way of ever being anywhere she is not — he says so at ch 290. **The Ledger is no longer
hers to order and there is nothing she can order instead.**

**What may not happen.** This close did not re-frame ch 239–298, did not re-title the volume, introduced no new
final enemy, and took nothing the ending owns — **the bargain, the fall, Feng's death, the Anchor, the
Sisterhood and the Scribe are untouched and belong to Volumes 11–16, the Scribe to Volume 15.** The year of
non-forgiveness was not retired, he was not forgiven, and she was not allowed to tell him to stop. `outline/series.md`
and `outline/ending.md` are byte-unchanged by this phase, and `outline/volume-06.md` does not exist.

### The Volume 6 hand-off, restated as a fact on the page and not as a plan

> She finished the books of the bound from the far side of a line — the claim answered where it could be
> answered, the family account settled, the brother's decade put where somebody has to look at it, three
> stages taken that no county heard — and the far side paid every one of them into books it keeps by hand.
> But past the last working point the country goes on, farmed and lit, with offices above the one office she
> knows the name of that have never needed anything from inside a bound until now — and one of them has just
> been handed a column that is hers, in a hand it can read, with a furnace behind it. The question is not
> what the bound was. **It is what the country past the line does with a person it can now read, who owes it
> hours, carries a furnace it did not build, and cannot go back inside where she was ever announced.**

**And alongside it, as facts at ch 298:** Golden Core 3, licensed by nothing and paid for out of far-side
wages · four fingers' worth of grip and an arm open half the time, and nothing offers · **a season over, a
column closed on the thirtieth in another man's hand, eighteen counterfoils in four marks and no nineteenth,
and three working days nobody will ever pay** · **entered as good in a book she cannot be taken out of** ·
**the claim answered and Hou Sanliang paid and never met** · **Yuan readable without her and the Ledger no
longer hers to order** · MEMBER ABSENT standing three times · the register still reading *reconciled* · the
Clay Plate still worn · Shen Xiu unforgiven and unthanked · the three sponsors silent · **the year still
running** · **the Hunter's request still pending and the line's own people still not knowing she exists** ·
**and a mill two miles east whose wheel has not turned since before she came, which she stood half a mile short
of and did not go past.**

### What the next phase is

**The Volume 6 planning phase.** Its prompt is `workspace/volume-05-close-record/PROMPT.md` and its whole job is
to **write `outline/volume-06.md`** — *The Real Realm*, ch 299–360, goal fixed by `outline/series.md` §9: *In
this volume, Ning Yan must survive what is on the other side, or the crossing was a death and not an escape* —
and to open Volume 6's planning. **`outline/volume-06.md` must not exist until that phase writes it, and this
close did not create a Volume 6 batch directory.**


## Who is where at end of chapter 283 — VOLUME 5 BATCH 0003 DRAFTED (superseded by the block above; kept as the record at ch 283)

**Day 580 — the twenty-seventh of a month. She is past the last working point, at the stone yard, booked to the end of the season, Golden Core 2, furnace twice lit, fifteen stubs.** Full record in `state/continuity.md` (*Established facts, Volume 5 Batch 0003* + running ledger at ch 283).

- **Ning Yan — GOLDEN CORE 2**, taken day 556 at her own order in the open where nothing is announced (ch 275, one bracket, second of volume). Cost: flat of second finger longer than eleven minutes, permanently; every writing hour costs its hour. Paper still says Foundation the first stage, never opened.
- **Body:** short breath four counts; right index gone, four fingers' grip; nine of eleven holds; turn gone; middle-bank method; right arm open a third; aperture cracked permanent; not hungry; cold like stone; **eighteen.**
- **What the batch changed:** authorisation onto far-side book against hours past the line (ch 268, cold, irreversible); return inside once, paper for Ning Bo placed, walked out (ch 271); Yuan entered as readable line away from her hearing (ch 272, *The Man the Board Keeps*), date of death-day gone, apology kept, Ledger as standing held stage ENDED; GC2 (ch 275); Ning Bo worked-not-settled; Ning Wu reachable-not-met; register read once as reconciled, never corrected.
- **Claim:** authorised onto far-side book, standing, moved, NOT answered (294–295 are Batch 0004's). Hou unpaid, never met; Lowwater date untouched; Luo Ping direction. **Next phase:** **the Volume 5 Batch 0004 drafting phase — ch 284–298 — prompt `workspace/volume-05-batch-0004/PROMPT.md`.**
- **Batch 0003 is repaired and its review findings are answered** (day table corrected to the pinned GC2 day 556; authorial scaffolding, the ending preview and the tier names cut from ch 272; the *He said one thing that night and it was present tense and it bought nothing* run broken across sixteen chapters; 22,738 words of body prose, average ~1,421, none under the 1,300 floor). Full record at the foot of `state/continuity.md` under ***Volume 5 Batch 0003 — REVIEW REPAIR***. **No beat, date, stage, title, bracket or planned event moved.**

## Who is where at end of chapter 267 — VOLUME 5 BATCH 0002 DRAFTED (superseded by the block above; kept as the record at ch 267)

Full record: `state/continuity.md` under ***Established facts, Volume 5 Batch 0002 (ch 253–267) — DEBT COMING DUE*** and ***Volume 5 running ledger — at end of ch 267***. **Day 521 — the twenty-eighth of a month. She is past the last working point of a line of work, in somebody's ordinary country, at a stone yard, booked to the end of the season, with a furnace of her own.**

- **Ning Yan** — **GOLDEN CORE 1, taken at her own order on day 512, in the open past the post, where nothing is announced.** Fifty days after Foundation 9 (day 462), forty-one after the volume's first day (day 471). The paper still says Foundation the first stage and **she has still never opened it.** The cost on the page: the turn at the end of a bar stroke is gone, permanently — one-handed bar work done — bought out of twenty-nine coppers of far-side material against booked pays.
- **Body at the last page.** Short breath **four counts everywhere. The right index finger gone, permanently; four fingers' worth of grip left on that hand.** Nine holds out of eleven in the left, two spent into a settled share and not coming back. Every county hour about eleven minutes with the flat of a second finger. Right arm open a third of the time. Aperture cracked through and permanent. **Not hungry since the first Foundation — and she has stopped buying bread, because the furnace took the bread money.** Cold the way a stone is cold. **Eighteen years old.**
- **What the fifteen chapters did.** **The road west reopened and Wei Ran gave the going once, ninety coppers, nine days hard, the coming unquoted** (253). **The office sent a second voice to the trestle and she refused out loud** (254). **IRREVERSIBLE: she took twenty-nine coppers of furnace material on promise against booked pays and closed the road behind her** (255). **A listed tending cleared by hand settled merit once, told before spent, two holds gone** (256). **She built the furnace base by hand** (257). **Golden Core 1, at her own order, where nothing is announced, one bracket, the turn gone** (258). **She learned the middle bank and took the heavy end back** (259). **A draft went west on a carrier's round with no date on it, moved and not answered** (260). **She rode the reopened road for a day and came back inside the fence, costing the day** (261). **A fair hand's Foundation copy went past the line into a far-side book, taken unknowing** (262). **A third voice at the store and a third refusal** (263). **News from nine days west by report, no second question** (264). **A slab on the four-fingered hand, nobody saw it; the ground warm where the furnace went in** (265). **Pay-day the twenty-seventh, eleven stubs, heavy end regained** (266). **The surface answer paid once, in a worker's mouth, in short sentences** (267).
- **The one thing the batch changed.** She is not a woman with wages any more. **She is a woman with a furnace no county licensed and no county can hear, three refusals standing, a draft nine days west, and a debt to a shed cleared — and the ground behind her hut holds what no book can take.**
- **The claim.** **Eight hundred jin, standing, entered in good order, travelling on the licence, ACTIONABLE ON THE FAR SIDE, moved a second time and not answered** — a draft west on a carrier's round with no date on it, office to office. **The price is still her column and she has said no three times.** Hou Sanliang unpaid, patient and never met. The date in Lowwater untouched. Luo Ping a direction.
- **Yuan.** Voice. **Present tense throughout, sparse, buying nothing except once:** at ch 256 he tells the price before spending and she says yes, and a share of a settled tending goes out of her foundation. **No figure of what is left of him anywhere in ch 253–267. Ledger hers to order and not ordered, not priced, not refused, not thought about.** The ch 272 sustained scene is Batch 0003's and nothing here touches it.
- **Inside the bound, by report only, once:** a hauler off the coming round at ch 264 — a tower lit at the fourth hour, a bundle on a stair, nine men still on the floor. **No scene west of the line in this batch.**
- **Pay and stubs.** **Pays on the seventeenth (day 510) and the twenty-seventh (day 520); a day not worked is a day not paid — the twenty-second stands as a gap. Eleven stubs in four marks in the oilcloth above the Clay Plate, none checkable within nine days of road.**
- **The register still reads *reconciled* (unmentioned). The Clay Plate still worn, undescribed, unremoved. MEMBER ABSENT stands three times, unraised. Shen Xiu unforgiven and unthanked. The three sponsors silent. Ning Wu does not appear. What became of the eleven is not raised. The ch 150 refusal stands. The year of non-forgiveness unmentioned and still carrying. Feng never on the page; no second Lung, no second appointment. Nothing above the Standing Office of the Second Account named.**
- **The surface answer IS paid — once, at ch 267, in a worker's mouth, in short sentences: being told and being charged were never the same thing.** The tenth part not given; the crews not proved wrong; the structural answer untouched for Volume 6.

## Who is where at end of chapter 238 — VOLUME 4 CLOSED AND AUDITED (cold reader starts here)

Full record: `state/continuity.md` under ***Established facts, Volume 4 complete (ch 179–238)*** and ***Volume 4 running ledger — at end of ch 238***. **Day 470 — the seventh of a month.** She is **on the far side of a line of work**, in a country where nothing is announced, at **Foundation 9**, with a column of hours that is hers in a wage-book on a trestle, and about four fingers on one hand.

- **Ning Yan** — **FOUNDATION 9, reached at her own order on day 462, the twenty-ninth of a month, announced in the open at the far end of the working ground, and the first announcement in four years that was heard past the line.** **There is nothing above nine anywhere in the world.** Ceiling on the front of the relicensed licence is still **Foundation the first stage** and **she has still never touched it**; seven conditions referenced and never re-listed; the claim the seventh and last thing on the paper. **The stage column in four county books reads one, two, three, five, six, seven, eight, nine** — one hole in nine lines, and the hole is hers and she put it there at ch 234.
- **Body at the last page.** Short breath **four counts everywhere — three is gone and does not come back.** **The order is gone out of her left hand**, which has the heel gone with it, and every grip is built from nothing, one at a time, before a bar lands. **Eleven holds that hold to the end of a stroke, out of about seventy tried since the fourth of a month four months back.** **THE RIGHT INDEX FINGER IS GONE, PERMANENTLY** — lost at the far end of the blind run at about the sixth hour on the twenty-first of a month, **the finger that makes the turn at the end of a stroke and the finger a licence nail goes in with**, and **four fingers' worth of grip left on that hand.** **Nothing in the book gives it back and nothing offers to.** Right arm open a third of the time. Aperture cracked through and permanent. Not hungry since the first Foundation. Cold the way a stone is cold. Bread from a store at five coppers a day.
- **What the last eight chapters did.** **The price was stated before the word and the word was said** (231, 233). **She squared two true books and gave up making a run out of them** (232). **She hired onto the stretch nobody is hired on, at a trestle, in daylight, with her name said out loud for the third time on that ground, and nobody persuaded** (233). **She suppressed Foundation 8 at her own order, and for the first time the absence landed on a board the far side keeps** (234). **She cleared fourteen days of the blind run by hand, the trial settled, the channel came back out of her foundation, and the finger went** (235). **The honest partial answer was said once against a board and he read the office name above this county in one breath and stopped** (236). **Foundation 9 went past the line, the far side wrote it down, and a claim of eight hundred jin was read out loud in a room that can act on it** (237). **She crossed, was certified, and wrote her name in a book on the far side** (238).
- **The two ways the boundary came down, both on the page and in this order.** **First the layer logged it:** a dated line went into a tally-board that was already on the far side before she got there, in a column that is hers, and **he could see the line going in and could not see any part of her on either side of it.** **Then her body:** three-quarters of a mile of the bad flat, a foot and a bit on the low side all the way, quiet ground that tells you nothing, four counts of breath, **four fingers on one hand and a bar in the other**, a day and a half, and one fall at about a third of the way along. **Nobody found her and nobody was sent. There is not going to be a third.**
- **The far side is ordinary country.** Settled, farmed, lit, **a trestle with a book on it, a fire under it, eleven men on it, and a building with a stove in it and six men in it.** Nobody there has ever heard a stage announced. Nobody there asked her a question. **A wage-book on the far side has her hours in it, in order, with dates, and a man at it tore a counterfoil off the book and put it face down on the plank — six stubs in an oilcloth now, in three marks, and none of them checkable against anything.**
- **The far-side board on a post.** Nine years of a narrow column nobody on this course fills, filled by a keeper off a cart from the far end of the reach, in a hand nobody within nine days of road can read. **At the last page it holds nine lines. Eight of them have something on them and one of them has nothing on it at all** — and **she has seen a column like that before and nobody else in that country ever has.**
- **What went into the space on the shelf (ch 238).** **The countersignature on her petition, in an office's hand and not a person's, dated from the far side.** **It is not an answer to the Hunter's request and the request is still pending.** A Wall writ is not issued to a field agent. **The line's own people still do not know she exists.**
- **Bai Zhu.** **Named once in the book, at ch 150, to a form, and on no document he signs.** At ch 231 he attended seven days, wrote three lines, bought nothing, threatened nothing, touched her, entered nothing, asked nothing, and said all of that in one breath. At ch 238 he **certified the crossing in a Warden's hand, signed by the office and not by a person** — his second document in the book and the same shape as his first — and said the whole of the rest of it in one breath again. **He did not ask her to come.**
- **The office above this county.** **Read at ch 236, in one breath, for the first time in four years: the Standing Office of the Second Account.** A middle office, with others above it. **He stopped there and nothing after ch 236 goes one notch further.** No Scribe. No Anchor. Nothing about what he was before he died.
- **The claim.** **Eight hundred jin, standing, the seventh and last condition, travelling on the licence.** A date is entered against it in Hou Sanliang's counting room in Lowwater and has not been torn off. **At ch 237 somebody on the far side read the seventh condition out loud in a room, which put an eight hundred jin standing claim into the first office within nine days of road that can act on it in its whole life. Not resolved. Hou Sanliang unpaid, patient and never met.**
- **Yuan.** Voice. **Two spends on the column of marks in the last eight chapters — ch 231, which bought the price, and the count is four marks on the back of a dead weight ticket; ch 233 spent the standing, not a mark, at a price of one day out of three, held under a day and put down when the crossing was over.** **Merit settled once, at ch 235, on a finished job and not on a death, and he intercepted a share and spent it out of her foundation while the base was one stage short of finished, and it did not come back.** **The channel is restored and is about six weeks at the last page**, said once, in weeks. **Ledger is hers to order at the last page and she did not order it.** **He still has not told her what he was before he died and she still has not told him she saw him as a readout.**
- **The year of non-forgiveness was touched once in this batch, at ch 230, at low volume** — she goes on using him on purpose, **and it is not forgiveness and the account still carries.** She does not forgive him and she does not tell him to stop.
- **MEMBER ABSENT stands three times and is permanent**, with the reason column a foot and a half wide and blank, and **the sheet went west for the last time this season.** No date west on any board after that man. The word was offered twice and no man has offered it a second time.
- **The course is closed against her and stays closed.** Two books in two hands, neither carrying a continuous run, **and three empty dates in the second one that are true and are never going to be filled.** Cui Bao said the whole of his position once and would do it again. **They are right and nothing in this volume proves them wrong.**
- **The register still reads *reconciled*. The Clay Plate still worn. Shen Xiu unforgiven and unthanked. The three sponsors silent. Ning Wu does not appear. What became of the eleven is not raised. The ch 150 refusal is not softened, deferred, reopened or regretted in any line.** Nothing about the sponsors' second book is lodged, copied or spoken anywhere in ch 119–238.
- **The crew at Qinghe is nine days west: a floor, nine men, and a tower with a light in it.** Said once, at ch 238, and not again.

### The four repairs, made in the chapters

**1 — ch 224, a named weekday.** *I would do the whole of it again on Friday* → *I would do the whole of it again on any working morning.* The rule is no weekday anywhere in ch 179–238; **the repair costs nothing, because nothing in the plot turns on the day of the week and *a working morning* is the idiom the volume already uses eight times.**

**2 — ch 232, a foreshadow of the finger, three chapters early.** *…and she had four fingers' worth of nothing on the other one that she had not noticed yet because nothing had been asked of that hand* → *…and every part of her that had gone wrong since the thaw had gone wrong in that hand.* The volume's rule is that nothing before ch 235 foreshadows the index finger. **The first form of this repair said *on that side*, which was wider than the body allows — the breath and the appetite both went, and neither is lateral. The close review scoped it to the hand.**

**3 — ch 179, the construction ch 235 needs, spent fifty-six chapters early.** *…because that is the finger that can still do a turn at the end of a stroke* → *…because a coin will go across the flat of that one and not across a fingertip.* The reason she used that finger is preserved; the phrase is not.

**4 — ch 220, the fifth stage on the wrong day of the month.** *…and it came on the nineteenth of this month* and *The stage came on the nineteenth* → *…and it came on the seventeenth of this month* and *The stage came at the fourth hour*, with *It came in the same week* → *It came earlier in that same day* and the two *two days* recast as *the rest of the day* and *the hours between the two of them*. **Day 360 is the seventeenth, the twenty-two days from the twenty-fifth of the month before land there, and the interval to day 386 is twenty-six only if the fifth is on the seventeenth. The chapter's second section heading is *The Seventeenth* and nine later chapters date the strike to the seventeenth, so the strike was never in doubt and the stage was the error.** The load-bearing argument of the chapter — that the two things are not connected — is preserved and in fact sharpens, because the two are now hours apart on one day rather than two days apart. **Corrected again at the close review: one sentence four paragraphs down still read *two things that happened in the same week*, and it now reads *two things that happened inside one day, hours apart*.**

**Nothing else in the volume was touched.** The planned plot, the sixty titles, the chapter order, the card beats, the four pinned stage days (386, 412, 440, 462), the pinned attendance day (389), both recorded deviations (ch 225 opening on the fifteenth, ch 228 running 412–414) and every ending stay where they were.

### Five findings recorded and not repaired, with the reason

1. **Bai Zhu's name is spoken twice more (ch 189, 196), against the outline's *named once*.** The operative prohibition — no document he signs carries his name — is intact, and both spoken uses are authorised by this volume's own batch cards. Repairing it would delete an authorised beat.
2. **The ch 234 cost is echoed at ch 236 and not at ch 235.** Ch 235 is the blind run, the trial, the finger and the telling. Putting the hole in it would fight every other thing the chapter does, and would make the cost a **third** naming rather than a second. Recorded, not repaired.
3. **Feng's fair hand copying the Foundation column, and his copies going past the line into a book the far side keeps, are not on the page** (row 16). He appears once, at ch 181, and Volume 4's own cards forbade a second appearance. Proposed repair carried unspent into Volume 5.
4. **No calendar month is named anywhere, but no weekday rule survives unwounded either** — repaired, not recorded, and listed here only so the record is complete: ch 224 was the sole instance and it is fixed.
5. **The far-side keeper gives her a knife and a spare strip at ch 236 *because she is holding her right hand in a way that made the tool look like a thing to be done one-handed*.** This is read as an outside man's eye catching something, and the narration does not tell the reader what it caught. Left as it is. Recorded because it is the only sentence in the volume that could be read as pricing the finger twice.

### The protagonist, answered in writing

**`outline/series.md` §2 names Yuan, male, as the protagonist and `state/book-metadata.md` records
Gender: Male, so the male-led requirement holds at series level. That was never the question. The question
is what Volume 4 did with him inside his own book, and here is the answer, in the only terms the book allows.**

**What he actually did across ch 179–238.**

He **found a hand that wrote a date against a claim.** Eight days out of ten, price said before the word, on a
mat with nine men asleep: Luo Ping, thirty-nine, a schedule-man, sixteen years, with a wife and a boy of
eleven living past the county's boundary, and she had to carry that and could not use it.

He **held a standing for a crossing and was paid for it out of a base that was not finished.** One day out of
three, stated at ch 231 before the word, taken at ch 233, held under a day, put down when the crossing was
over, and spent out of a foundation one stage short of done.

He **intercepted a share of a settled job and spent it.** The job was the blind run; the trial cleared; the
Merit came on a finished thing and not on a dead man; he took a share of it and spent it and it did not come
back, and it cost her the right index finger.

He **restored his own life.** About six weeks at the last page, in the only unit the book uses for it.

And he **read one name he had said four times he could not read, in one breath, and stopped.**

**Every one of those is a decision by the book's protagonist. Every one of them was paid for by the woman, in
hours, in a stage, or in a finger.**

**Is that enough? No — and here is the finding, stated plainly rather than softened.**

**Sixty chapters of close third on a woman who is not the protagonist, in which the protagonist is a voice,
off-panel, rationed to four marks and then to one, is a structural debt, not a stylistic one.** The male-led
requirement is met at series level and will be met at every volume level, because Yuan is the man the whole
book is about — the layer, the cost, the thing that pays, the thing that is rationed. But **Volume 4 is the
second consecutive volume in which the man has no scene, no body, no room and no other character to speak to,
and the debt is now two volumes of sixteen old.** The batch that wrote it knew: the open question was carried
in `state/open-threads.md` from the Batch 0004 planning review onward and **nothing in the drafting of any of
the four batches settled it.**

**What Volume 5 does about it, decided here, before Volume 5 opens.**

The options the series outline permits are all still open. **Volume 5 takes the third: *the shape of Volume 5
is itself the correction*, and it takes it inside Volume 5's own sixty chapters rather than by re-framing
Volume 4.**

Stated as a requirement the Volume 5 planner must meet:

- **Volume 5 must put Yuan on the page in his own right for at least one sustained scene** — not more rationed
  sentences, not a description of him, not a dream. **A scene where he acts, or is acted on, away from her
  hearing.** He has never been in a room she was not in; there is now a country on the far side of the line
  where a man who keeps a board asks no questions, and a claim that has landed in the first office able to
  act on it, and the machinery of the layer is the only thing in the world that has ever moved him.
- **The cost of that scene is not optional and is not hers to pay again.** He has already paid the finger and
  the foundation. Whatever Volume 5 puts him in, it takes something that is his and that the ending still
  needs — because `outline/ending.md` is fixed that he refuses the bargain and takes the fall and is gone
  before ch 1000, and **a man the reader has never seen in a room cannot carry a fall.**
- **What Volume 5 may not do:** re-frame ch 179–238, re-title the volume, introduce a new final enemy, or take
  anything Volume 5 owns — the family account, Ning Wu's decade, the four months erased in Chapter 3, the
  bargain and the fall. **The Scribe is fixed in `outline/ending.md` and belongs to Volume 15**, and this
  decision does not touch it.
- **What Volume 5 must carry unchanged:** close third on Ning Yan for every external scene; **every sentence
  Yuan speaks in the present tense, and no other character ever in a marked present frame**; the fifteen
  continuity facts above; and the standing that his Ledger is **hers to order** and she has not ordered it.

**What it costs:** the volume's first instinct will be to put him on the page as a resource again — more
sentences, a bigger ration, a longer channel. **That is not the correction.** The correction is a scene in
which he is a person with his own situation and is not being spent. **It costs Volume 5 the easiest shape it
had, and it is worth it, because the alternative is a book with no protagonist in it from ch 179 onward.**

### The Volume 5 hand-off — Volume 5 is *Mortal Ash*, ch 239–298

> **SUPERSEDED AS STANDING POSITION BY `## Who is where at end of chapter 252`, which is above it in this
> file. This block is kept because it is what Volume 5's planner opened on and because its escalation
> sequence and its *New question for Volume 6* are still binding. Batch 0001 (ch 239–252) is on the page;
> Batch 0002 (ch 253–267) is the next thing to draft.**

> **In this volume, Ning Yan must finish the world they came from, or leaving it costs everyone she left standing in it.**

**The question Volume 4 opens onto it, as a fact on the page and not as a plan:**

> **She stood in the stretch nobody is paid to tend, and an announcement came out of her and went past the line, and the people on the far side wrote it in their own board because that is what the board is for. The bound did not notice, and cannot: a county book has no column for a thing heard past its own boundary, and nine counties that heard nine stages in order have heard nothing else. But the far side keeps a wage-board for a line it maintains by hand, and it now holds a figure in it that no county in nine counties can answer for — and a woman standing on their side of it whose licence carries a claim their side can act on.**

**And alongside it, as standing fact and not as plan:**

- **She is at FOUNDATION NINE, the top of the second realm, and there is nothing above nine anywhere in the world.** The paper still says Foundation the first stage on the front of it, **and she has still never touched the relicensed licence.** The seventh condition is the claim and the last thing on the paper.
- **She has lost the right index finger, permanently, at the blind run, and the crew were told out loud.** Four fingers' worth of grip left on that hand. Eleven holds in the other one out of about seventy tried. **Nothing gives it back and nothing offers to.**
- **She is across.** A wage-book on the far side with a column of hours that is hers, in order, with dates, kept by people who are not a county and are not obliged to keep it. **Nobody on that line has asked her a question.**
- **The Hunter's request is still pending.** The space took a countersignature at ch 238 and **that is not an answer**, and **a Wall writ is not issued to a field agent**, and **the line's own people still do not know she exists.**
- **The claim has moved and is not resolved.** Eight hundred jin, read out loud in a room on the far side, in the first office within nine days of road that can act on it. **Hou Sanliang is unpaid, patient and never met.**
- **About six weeks of channel**, restored by a listed trial cleared rather than by a killing, **out of a base that was one stage short of finished when he spent it.** **Ledger is hers to order and she has not ordered it.**
- **MEMBER ABSENT stands three times and is permanent**, with a reason column a foot and a half wide and blank, and **the sheet went west for the last time this season.** **No date west on any board after that man.**
- **The register still reads *reconciled*. The Clay Plate is still worn. Shen Xiu is unforgiven and unthanked. The three sponsors are silent. Feng is alive, warm, column at five, and not thanked. Ning Wu does not appear. What became of the eleven is not raised. She does not forgive him and she does not tell him to stop.**
- **And the unspent item this audit added: Feng's fair hand copying the Foundation column, and his copies going up a stair that now belongs to a district clerk and past the line into a book the far side keeps.** Volume 4's own cards forbade him a second appearance and the beat was never given. **It is available, and it belongs to the far side, which is where Volume 5 starts.**

### Close review repair — the Volume 4 close, audited after the fact

**A review ran against the finished close and reproduced its arithmetic: batches 28,607 / 31,900 / 28,581 /
31,484 = 120,572, Volume 3 at 135,014, and `((360 − 134) mod 30) + 1 = 17` with 386 − 360 = 26. Markdown, titles
and the bracketed-line budget came back clean, the scope of the close was clean, and the four pinned stage days,
the pinned attendance day, both recorded deviations and all sixty endings were confirmed untouched. Five
findings came out of it. Four were real and are repaired; the fifth is the controller's.**

1. **ch 220 kept the old timing in one sentence.** *They were two things that happened in the **same week***,
   four paragraphs below the sentences the close had just moved into one day. **Now *They were two things
   that happened inside one day, hours apart*.** Nothing else in the chapter carries the old span.
2. **ch 232's repair claimed more than the body allows.** *every part of her that had gone wrong since the thaw
   had gone wrong on that side* is contradicted by two losses that are not lateral — the breath down to four
   counts and the loss of appetite, both established at ch 214. **Now *…had gone wrong in that hand***, which
   is what the fact supports and still scopes the sentence away from the finger lost at ch 235.
3. **The weekday rule was inferred, unenforced outside one volume, and broken eight times.** It is now **canon
   in `bible/world.md`** — *Rules of the world that hold everywhere* and *What must never appear* — and also
   world rule 9 in `state/continuity.md`: **the world has no named weeks; narration may not name a weekday,
   from any register and from any mouth.** The eight standing instances in closed Volumes 1 and 2 were repaired
   at word level: **ch 40** · **ch 47** · **ch 104** · **ch 109** · **ch 111 (×2)** · **ch 114 (×2)**. **A grep of
   `chapters/` for all seven weekday names now returns zero across ch 1–238.** **Reopening closed chapters is
   not new to this book — Volume 3's Batch 0001 review reopened two Volume 2 chapters, ch 116 and ch 118, over a
   date that contradicted the channel's own figure. **This is the first reopening that exists to apply a canon
   rule across volumes**, because the rule had never been written down anywhere. **Volume 2's recorded file set
   is unchanged at 111,226** on its own raw-count method, because the repairs replaced as many words as they
   removed; Volume 4's moves from 120,571 to **120,572**.
4. **Two records were wrong about the text, and both are corrected in place.** `state/book-metadata.md`
   described the word-count method as excluding *`##` section headings* when the figures also exclude the `#`
   chapter title line, and it claimed ch 179 was *twenty-two words over* the 2,700 guide on a raw count; by the
   method the file itself states, ch 179 is **2,683** and ch 199 is 2,661, so nothing in Volume 4 is over the
   guide. **One reviewer claim was itself wrong and is not adopted: it said no heading called *The Seventeenth*
   existed in ch 220. The chapter title is *Struck Out* and the section heading *The Seventeenth* is at line 9;
   the audit's original citation was loose about which it was, and is now written precisely.**
5. **Operational, and deliberately not fixed here: `workspace/volume-04-close/` has no `.done`.** The close is
   finished, every other phase directory in the repository carries the marker, and `novels.yml` gates dispatch on
   it, so an audited close is still eligible and will re-run. **`PHASE_SYSTEM.md` says only the controller may
   mark a phase done, so this phase has not written it. This is for the controller or the operator.**

**Carried deliberately and flagged: the protagonist correction is wholly deferred into Volume 5.** Yuan is the
named protagonist, has been a voice only and off-panel for two volumes of sixteen, and `outline/ending.md`
fixes that he takes the fall and is gone before ch 1000. The requirement is now a hard constraint on Volume
5's shape — **at least one sustained scene in which he acts, or is acted on, away from her hearing, and it
costs him something the ending still needs.** It is load-bearing on a phase that has not started. **Do not let
Volume 5 open without it.**

**The Volume 5 planning phase re-ran against work it had already completed. `outline/volume-05.md` (278
lines as it stood before the three repairs below; **318 lines as it stands now**) and
`workspace/volume-05-batch-0001/PROMPT.md` (72 lines, unchanged) were already on disk, and

`outline/volume-04.md` and the audited state files rather than rewriting them. Nothing was rewritten. No
chapter in Volumes 1–4 was opened. No batch card was written and no second prompt was created.**

**The deliverables are complete against the phase's own list.** `outline/volume-05.md` carries the title,
the range, the goal sentence verbatim from `outline/series.md` §9, the Volume 4 question verbatim as a fact
on the page, the antagonist from §6 item 4 and not Volume 4's Wall, the surface answer owned and bounded
against the structural answer, the starting state read off this file, the protagonist section with the ch
272 scene named and its price named in advance, the three inherited structural facts, a Golden Core 1–3
ladder with days and intervals and a re-derived announcement rule, a four-batch pressure rotation with no

forward, the sizing rule, and the do-nots.

### Three defects found and repaired in `outline/volume-05.md`

1. **THE FOUR MONTHS ERASED IN CHAPTER 3 WERE NAMED IN VOLUME 5, AND THAT CONTRADICTS THE FIXED ENDING.**
   The file named them in three places — the Batch 0003 row of the pressure rotation, *Concrete resolution*
   at ch 296, and required continuity fact 16. **`outline/ending.md` §8 is fixed and is the authority: *The
   four months erased in Chapter 3 are named once, in the final chapter, as a loss that is never recovered
   and is not forgiven.*** **All three now read carried and not named**, and a hard rule was added so no
   drafter spends it. **Why `outline/ending.md` wins:** it opens *This file is the authority for the end of
   the book. Nothing in it may be rewritten later in the run*, and the close review's second binding rule is
   that **absolutes in this book are literal** — *named once* is an absolute, and naming them here as well
   would make it false against the manuscript. **Volume 5 loses nothing load-bearing: its promise is the
   claim, the family account, the furnace and the leaving, and none of the four runs on the four months.
   Ch 1000 keeps the only naming it was ever going to get.**
2. **An unsourced figure.** *Concrete resolution* read **sixty-two days of mortal ash on her boots**.
   Sixty-two is Feng's age and is used for him twice elsewhere in the same file; the volume runs day 471 to
   about day 620, which is about five months. **Now reads about five months of far-side ash on her boots.**
3. **The volume's clock opened on day 470 in two places.** Day 470 is where Volume 4 closed and ch 239 opens
   on **day 471**, as `outline/batches/volume-05-batch-0001.md` pins at its own clock block (Batch 0001 ends
   day 506, Batch 0002 opens day 507). **Both now read day 471**, and the *Geography* block carries all three
   pins so the outline and the card file cannot drift apart again.

**`workspace/volume-05-batch-0001/PROMPT.md` was given one addition:** the four-months do-not is now in its
*Carry forward* list, so a planning model that reads only the prompt cannot spend ch 1000's beat.

### One finding recorded and NOT repaired, because it is not this phase's to fix

**`state/open-threads.md` line 33 gives the wrong chapter for the relicensing's third leg.** It reads
**"Vol 4 — ANSWERED IN LEG THREE, ch 266"** and **"The answer came at ch 266 on day 266"**, where
**day 266 is right and the chapter is not.** Canon is **ch 201** for the countersignature, delivered on the
licence at **ch 207** — confirmed three ways: `state/current.md` close-audit row 1 (*clearance ch 198 →
the sponsors' seal ch 200 → countersignature ch 201*), `outline/batches/volume-04-batch-0002.md` (*Leg three
is an office's hand on day 266*, with the three legs pinned at 198 / 200 / 201), and the audit's
*Established facts* block. **The duplication of 266 in both the chapter and the day slot is the signature of
a stale edit.** **PROPOSED REPAIR, not made here:** in that one row, **ch 266 → ch 201**, on both
occurrences, leaving *day 266* as it stands. `state/open-threads.md` is not a file this phase was asked to
edit and a closed state file is not silently amended on a planning run.

### A standing conflict this run should know about, recorded once

**Three closed files say the four months erased in Chapter 3 are Volume 5's own thing** —
`outline/volume-04.md` (*Volume 5's own things — the family account, Ning Wu's decade, the four months
erased in Chapter 3*), `state/current.md` line 202, and `state/open-threads.md` line 197 — **and
`outline/ending.md` §8 says they are named once in the final chapter.** **The reading in all three is the
wrong one and is corrected above and at source.** `state/continuity.md` line 1629 does not make the claim
either way; it lists the four months under *Held for Volume 5 **or later***, which is correct and stands.

## Volume 5 planning close review — findings applied

`logs/volume-04-close-record.review.log` reviewed commits `b528f08` → `d4f4784` and returned seven findings.
**Four were faults in the files and are repaired at source. Three are process notes about the pipeline and
are recorded here and nowhere else, because the files that own them are controller-owned.** **No planned
plot moved. No card, chapter, title, day, batch split, cost, antagonist beat, the ch 272 scene or anything
in `outline/ending.md` changed.** Every edit below is a number that was wrong, a figure with no day behind
it, a sentence that contradicted itself, or a stale line count.

### Repaired at source

1. **F1 — an arithmetic error the repair itself introduced.** `outline/volume-05.md` gave Golden Core 1
   (ch 258, day 512) as *"50 days after Foundation 9 (day 462); 42 days after the volume's first day"*.
   **512 − 471 = 41**, and the forty was the discredited pre-repair interval re-anchored rather than
   removed. Now reads **41 days after the volume's first day (day 471)**, which is 512 − 462 = 50 and
   512 − 471 = 41, both true. **Day 512 was never in dispute.** The card file's flag, which had certified
   the wrong pair as *"both true"*, is corrected with it.
2. **F2 — the review fix blessed a wrong absolute.** *About four seconds* appears **eight** times in
   ch 179–238, not nine: **ch 181 (×2), 191, 198, 205, 208 (×2), 212**, and ch 198 is one prose line and
   never a section heading. Batch attribution is **three in Batch 0001, four in Batch 0002, one in Batch
   0003, none in Batch 0004**, none in the volume's last sixteen chapters. Corrected in **four** places
   that carried the nine: `outline/volume-05.md` hard rules, `outline/batches/volume-05-batch-0001.md`
   traps block and flags, `state/continuity.md` close-audit correction 1, and **row 18 of the close-audit
   table above**, which is where the figure was first asserted. **A fifth copy sits in
   `workspace/volume-04-close-record/PROMPT.md` and has deliberately been left there**: it is this phase's own
   dispatched instruction, already executed, and it is not a file a writer run amends. The prompt the next
   phase will actually read, `workspace/volume-05-batch-0001/PROMPT.md`, carries no count, and both files on
   its mandatory read list — `state/continuity.md` and this one — are now right.
3. **F3 — the ch 243 card contradicted itself on age.** *Resistance* gave **Wei Ran** as twenty and the
   card's *Continuity fact to preserve* read *"the book does not romanticise that she is eighteen."* Canon is
   **Wei Ran twenty, Ning Yan eighteen**. The batch's own character list repeated the dropped subject. **The
   subject is restored at both sites and each now names which woman the age belongs to.** The register of
   the card — a rate, a road and not a favour — is untouched.
4. **F4 — four durations with no day behind them, in a book where absolutes are literal.** Her crossing
   date is nowhere given, so *"went through without permission **four months ago**"* is **dropped** and now
   says she has never said when, which is what ch 222 does. *"Has not been able to ask a question for
   **four months**"* is **pinned to the fourteenth, ch 229, day 417**, the last time a road back inside was
   put in front of her, with the interval given as a card fact and expressly kept off the page, which is the
   rule the batch already runs on. *"True for **two months**"*, twice — the five-coppers wage is **canon at
   ch 238, day 470**, and Batch 0001 opens on day 471, so the arithmetic is **days** old; both are
   re-anchored to ch 238 and neither carries a duration. A fifth, *"What **four months** of Volume 4
   bought"*, matched no span in the volume and now reads *the back half of Volume 4*, which is what ch 221
   and ch 232 are.

### Recorded, not acted on, because the files are not this run's to change

- **F5 — two phases now own the same deliverable.** The planning phase's prompt says the batch cards are the
  next phase's job, and the review-fix pass nonetheless edited the next phase's card file. That is
  reconciliation and it was defensible; the cost is that both phases own one file, and F2 and F3 were the
  residue. **The residue is now cleared.** Which phase writes the cards is dispatch, and dispatch is
  controller-owned.
- **F6 — the next phase ran out of order.** `workspace/volume-05-batch-0001/` carries `.deferred`,
  `.attempts: 1` and `.retry-after`, and its card file was committed at 19:53, before the planning phase
  finished at 20:09, so it was drafted against the pre-repair outline. Per `PHASE_SYSTEM.md`, `.deferred`
  means rate-limited, not failed. **The outcome is reconciled and is now verified against the repaired
  outline**, which is the thing that mattered.
- **F7, first half — a stale figure in this file.** It described `outline/volume-05.md` as 278 lines; it is
  318. **Now reads 278 as it stood before the three repairs and 318 as it stands now**, so the statement
  is true in both readings.
- **F7, second half — `state/phase-ledger.json` is absent from the repository.** `AGENTS.md` and
  `PHASE_SYSTEM.md` both name it and both forbid editing it. **It is presumed to be created at runtime by
  the workflow, and it has deliberately not been created here.** A hand-written ledger is a worse fault
  than an absent one, and it would be controller-owned the moment it existed.

**Read this block before using the Golden Core intervals, the four-seconds count or any elapsed duration in
`outline/batches/volume-05-batch-0001.md`.** The Volume 5 planning is otherwise closed and the batch cards
are the next phase's.
