## Who is where at end of chapter 230 — VOLUME 4 BATCH 0004, FIRST HALF, CLOSED (cold reader starts here)

Full record: `state/continuity.md` under ***Established facts, Volume 4 Batch 0004 first half (ch 223–230)***. **Day 426 — the twenty-third of a month.** She is past the last stake, in a hut with three boards in it, **at Foundation 7**, with **five empty dates' worth of ground behind her that no book on earth holds**, about five coppers, five counterfoils and a licence she has never touched.

- **Ning Yan** — **FOUNDATION 7, reached on day 412 at her own order, announced in the ordinary way, and taken while she was three days out of every book.** Ceiling on the front of the relicensed licence is still **Foundation the first stage** and she has **still never touched it**; seven conditions referenced and never re-listed; the claim the seventh and last. **The stage column in four county books reads one, two, three, five, six, seven, nine, with a space above the row she is standing in, and nine days of road away nobody has been told.** **Body:** short breath **four counts everywhere — three is gone and does not come back**. **The order is gone out of her left hand**, which has the heel gone as well, and every grip is built from nothing, one at a time, before a bar lands. **Eleven holds that hold to the end of a stroke, out of about sixty tried**, kept on the back of a dead weight ticket with the flat of a second finger at eleven minutes a line. **The right index finger is whole and is not named, priced or foreshadowed anywhere in ch 223–230.** Right arm open a third of the time. Aperture cracked through and permanent. **Not hungry since the first Foundation**, cold at the fire and out of it, and **not in the cook-fire at all — her own bread at the store, five coppers.**
- **What this half of the batch did.** Four costs, none of them the same thing and all four named once in new words: **the sixth stage is that the sound in their ears becomes a line on a board and the people on the line can tell a stage from a bill (ch 224); the seventh is three days that no book can find, and eleven men who stand a stretch for nothing (ch 228).** **The situation is visibly different from ch 222:** the course is closed against her, the sub-contractor's book has **three empty dates in it that are true and will stay empty**, the county's member sheet has gone west **for the last time this season** with MEMBER ABSENT on it a third time and the reason column a foot and a half wide and blank, she holds **five counterfoils in two contractors' marks instead of four in one**, she has stood **inside the bad flat** and knows what is on it, and **she has decided to hold the Ledger for the crossing and has said neither the word nor the price.**
- **The blind run is not entered.** She has seen it from a rise, walked it with a cart, gone down into the low side on one knee, and found the two men in the ground with a bar laid across them. **She has not hired onto it and no card before ch 233 may let her.** Ch 233 hires her in daylight at a trestle.
- **Foundation 8 is twenty-eight days out, at day 440, ch 234, suppressed at her own order with no bracketed line in the chapter at all.** Foundation 9 is at day 462, ch 237, and is the one that goes past the line.
- **The Hunter has not appeared.** He is a man in a plain coat with a notebook shut against his side, seen at two hundred yards across the ground on the twenty-third, not writing. **He is named nowhere in ch 223–230 and no document he signs carries a name.** He certifies the crossing at ch 238.
- **The office above this county is not named, not asked for, and he is silent about it.** He reads it at ch 236 and not one page earlier.
- **The far-side board is not touched in ch 223–230** and its last column with one of her dates in it is exactly as ch 216 left it.
- **Yuan.** Voice. **Two ration spends in eight chapters — ch 223, which bought nothing at all, and ch 230, which bought him the word *not today* and three sentences in return.** **Four marks on the back of a dead weight ticket and the count has not moved between the tenth of the month before and the twenty-second. No figure of what is left is stated anywhere in ch 223–230.** **Ledger NOT ordered in ch 223–230 — the decision is taken in her own head on the twenty-second and the word is not said and the price is not said.** The standing is not explained and the refusal is not run again. **Merit: none. Nothing in ch 223–230 kills anything or clears a listed trial and the channel does not come back.** **He does not grow back and he is quieter at the end of this half than at the start.**
- **The year of non-forgiveness was touched once, at low volume, at ch 230** — she goes on using him on purpose, in the plainest terms, **and it is not forgiveness and the account still carries.** She does not forgive him and she does not tell him to stop.
- **The course is closed against her and stays closed.** Cui Bao came out past the wire once, on the fourteenth, in the afternoon, and said the whole of it: **the referral was correct, he acted on it, he would do it again on Friday, and he heard her stage go on the thirteenth and wrote none of it down.** Nine men and a girl of nineteen went out on three days of a stretch that is on their board, unpaid, and told her nothing, and she found out from a plank at forty feet.
- **The sub-contractor is not the course and has become a person.** About forty-five, four columns and a painted mark, and he has now squared his book with three empty dates in it and said, without looking up: *a hole you cannot see is worse than one you can.* **He does not know what she is and does not ask.**
- **Pei, about sixty, a carrier, NEW MINOR (ch 225).** He is the hand that writes the attendance column and he has carried her sheet three months. **He offered her the word once himself, on an earlier round of his, and has not offered it a second time** — in the chapter he says only that the column is not his to fill because it travels down the same road as her hours, and that he will not say it again until he has to write in it; **he did not ask her a question about it, because in a year he does not want to be a man who asked one.** He carries oil and salt and about eleven coppers' worth of trade and **he took the last strip of paper with her name on it out of that reach on the sixteenth and there is no date west on any board after him for the rest of the season.**
- **The woman at the store between the last stake and the cart turn, NEW MINOR (ch 226).** She has never asked anything. She has thought about the fact that there is a woman coming out of that line without a pot, put the bread down on the other side of the salt, told her to stop standing in the middle of the floor, and did not call it an offer. **Not a thread.**
- **Wei Ran appears once, at ch 229, and goes off page.** She came past the trestle with one team and a load, said the correct thing — he would not give a better stretch and he would think about it for a day — and offered **twenty coppers to be set down past the cart turn and picked up on the way back**, which is a rate and a road and not a favour. **She then said the true thing about the bad flat in the ordinary voice: she has taken two loads that way in four years and she went round both times, and about half the people who look at ground nobody hires on go.** **Nothing is a romance. Nothing is a thread.**
- **The bad flat, learned on the page (ch 227).** Three-quarters of a mile, **no mark, no stake and no board at any point on it.** Drift a foot and a bit on the low side all the way, thickest where the work was worst. **No ring anywhere in it** — the low side answers every turn of a boot and does not announce which way it is going, and it does not tell you, which is the danger. **Two men in the ground about four inches deep with a bar laid across them, drift closed over the both of them, and the bar still there.** No board, no name, no wage, no man who stands a stretch on it. **The shortage there was never strength; it is how long a person can be wrong in it and not go in.** She went down into it on one knee on the first of the month and told nobody but the reader.
- **MEMBER ABSENT is on the sheet for the sixteenth, for the third time, permanent**, in a column of its own, with **a foot and a half of blank paper in the reason column going west for the last time this season.** She did not write the word because the reason column is the column her hours go in, **and she never says the reason aloud — it is given once in narration and not in her mouth.** **The word has been offered twice and no man has offered it a second time** — the decent man at a trestle inside the wire offered it once and did not offer it again, and **Pei offered it once himself on an earlier round of his** and in this scene says only that it is not his to fill because it travels down the same road as her hours; **he does not ask her a question about it, because in a year he does not want to be a man who asked one.**
- **Two books in two hands and neither carries a run**, and this half of the batch made that worse rather than better: **three empty dates in the sub-contractor's book that are true and are never going to be filled, because a book that keeps hours that were not worked on the stretch it names is a book that lies.**
- **Five counterfoils in the oilcloth above the Clay Plate** — four in one contractor's mark and **the first one in the world in a different mark, from a man who is not answerable to the man who struck her name out of a book.** A mark nobody checks is worth what the man who makes it is worth, and it goes in a coat and is kept, and **not one of the five can be compared with either book.**
- **Nothing Volume 3 left open was answered.** The claim stands with its date; Hou Sanliang is not met; what became of the eleven is not raised; the office above this county is not named; the sponsors' second book is lodged nowhere; **Shen Xiu unforgiven and unthanked; the register still reads *reconciled*; the Clay Plate still worn; Ning Wu does not appear.** The ch 150 refusal is not softened, deferred, reopened or regretted in any line, and no line lets her wonder what she would have done. **The Hunter's request is still pending** and the empty space has not been answered in any form.
- **The crew at Qinghe is nine days west and is not mentioned in ch 223–230.**

---

## Who is where at end of chapter 222 — VOLUME 4 BATCH 0003 CLOSED (SUPERSEDED by the block above; kept as the cold-reader record Batch 0004 opened on)

Full record: `state/continuity.md` under ***Established facts, Volume 4 Batch 0003 (ch 209–222)*** and
***Volume 4 running ledger — at end of ch 222***. **Day 376 — the third of a month.** She is **outside the
wire**, on ground that is nobody's job, past the last stake, at the sub-contractor's rate, with about ten
coppers, four stubs of paper in an oilcloth, and a licence she has never touched.

- **Ning Yan** — **FOUNDATION 5**, reached at her own order on day 360 and announced in the ordinary way.
  **Four stages in one season, four brackets, four costs, and not one of them the same thing.** Ceiling on
  the front of the relicensed licence is still **Foundation the first stage** and the relicensed licence is
  **still a document she has never touched**; seven conditions referenced and never re-listed; the claim the
  seventh and last. **The stage column in four county books reads one, two, three, five, six, seven, nine,
  with a space above the row she is standing in, and nine days of road away nobody has been told.**
  **Body at the last page:** short breath **four counts on the flat, on the line and on a grade — three is
  gone and does not come back**. **The order is gone out of her left hand** — she can hold, carry and pull,
  and every grip now has to be built from nothing, one at a time, before the bar lands. **The heel of her
  left hand is still gone and that is not what this cost; every county hour still takes eleven minutes with
  the flat of a second finger, unchanged.** Right arm open a third of the time. Aperture cracked through and
  permanent. **Not hungry since the first Foundation**, and cold at the fire and out of it, and **she is not
  in the fire at all since the middle of the month before** — her own bread at the store, six coppers then
  five.
- **The course is closed against her, lawfully and correctly.** A rule nine years old, used twice, right
  both times: **a course may close its own working ground against a person it does not carry.** The reason
  was **the Hunter's own referral, read out of his notebook at a table by him**, which says the thing in her
  head is unauthorised and is carried by a licensee in good standing; both are on the same paper and both
  are true. It reached her on the eighth of a month at about the sixth hour at a trestle, in nine words, in
  the ordinary voice, and **she said nothing at all in her own defence.**
- **What it cost them, and it is on the ground and not in a speech.** The stretch she kept went back in
  **three days** and the ring is loud again inside a week. The run is shorter, **the contract is smaller**,
  **the board has a hole in it** where a mark used to get a figure, **nine men are idle** and **the girl of
  nineteen is idle and she is somebody's daughter.** They are right and nothing in this volume proves them
  wrong.
- **The Hunter's win, and he does not want it.** He walked nine days east because his order is executable
  here and nowhere else on earth, and on the eleventh of a month he said, in the ordinary voice, in front of
  her, that **he does not want it executed and wants the answer pending**, because a pending answer is a
  question somebody above him has to answer and he has never believed the answer would be the right one. He
  bought nothing, threatened nothing, touched her, entered nothing, asked no question and said so in the
  same breath. **He is named nowhere in this batch and no document he signs carries a name.**
- **Her name is struck out of a contractor's wage-book**, in a foreman's own hand, in the open, at the
  second hour of the seventeenth of a month, with a stroke of a pen. **The hours to that day stand.** The
  rate stops. The book does not close.
- **The batch's irreversible decision, ch 221.** She hires on under a **sub-contractor** past the last
  stake: **five coppers a day and a place at the fire**, his own book with four columns and a painted mark,
  a man who is not answerable to the foreman and **who does not know why the course closed and does not
  ask.** Her name said out loud for the second time on that ground, in daylight, nobody persuaded.
  **From that hour two books in two hands hold her name and neither is answerable to the other, and the
  hours split, so neither carries a continuous run — which is the exact instrument the whole relicensing
  was built out of.** She took it apart herself and did not do the arithmetic out loud.
- **What she knows that nobody else does, and has told nobody (ch 213).** Thick drift under a bar **makes a
  noise and keeps making it after the bar stops**, for about as long as it takes to count eleven, and eleven
  men and a girl have an ordinary working word for it — *a stretch that has got loud.* Over sixteen days she
  kept what the board said against what she remembers of what nine counties have heard, in order, over four
  years: **the loud stretches are nearest the inside end, the worst is the worst-worked, and the quietest
  ground on nine days is at the far end, which is where there is nothing to announce because there is nobody
  there.** She said it to herself once, in one plain sentence, and built no theory on it. **It cannot be
  proved, because the only control in the world is herself.** She told nobody, including him, and she has
  not moved since.
- **The last column on the board (ch 216).** At the far end of the far plank, past the last stretch the
  crew works, there is a narrower column with no heading she can read and **nothing on this course fills
  it.** It is filled by a strip of the same cloth, off a cart from the far end of the working reach,
  **nine years**, a hand from past the last stake, a cart that is not identified on that course. **She can
  read the frame and not one word inside it**, and **one line in it is dated the thirteenth of this month —
  the day she took the second Foundation — and is in a form none of the nine years around it is in.** She is
  the only person within nine days of road who can tell that line from a reading, **she told nobody she had
  told the difference, and she did not say out loud what the line is.** **Her hand was on the Ledger on the
  evening of that day and she did not order it** — one sentence of narration, no dialogue, no price, nobody
  told.
- **The district's answer space took its first entry (ch 210).** A carrier came back east on the third of a
  month with the county's round. **A date and one office word: *Held.*** It means the paper is in a room and
  nothing has been done to it. **She heard a second meaning in it, she is the only person within nine days
  who could, and she told nobody and did not put it in a sentence with a verb even in her own head.**
  **The request is still pending. The referral is still pending. Nothing was answered.**
- **MEMBER ABSENT** is on the sheet for the sixteenth, for the second time, **in a column of its own**, with
  a foot and a half of blank paper in the reason column that she chose and that is going nine days west
  blank. **She did not write the word because the reason column is the column her hours go in.** Nothing
  about it is softened, requalified or converted, and **Ma Sheng did not offer it a second time** — he read
  her line at the trestle and went and wrote a man's hours against a stretch, and that was the whole of it.
- **The road west has closed for the season (ch 210).** The carrier came back on the third and the next one
  is a man who runs his own book and leaves when he leaves, **and after him there is no date west on that
  board for the rest of the season.** She stood at the trestle when he went anyway, at the fourth hour, on a
  working morning, because that is where she is at the fourth hour.
- **Wei Ran, twenty, mortal-born**, a carter with one team out past the far end of the working reach and
  back. **She is the first person Ning Yan has met who went through without permission.** Asked one direct
  question, she said so in four words and did not elaborate, and she said the correct thing about the line
  as a fact and not as comfort. **She goes off page at the end of ch 222 and nothing is opened that Batch
  0004 does not honour.** Nothing in Volume 4 is a romance.
- **The far end (ch 222).** She paid a working day's rate — five coppers — to be set down in a field past
  the last stake. **The drift is thin, there is no ring, and a bar into six inches of it does not ring.**
  The quietest ground on nine days is the ground where there is nobody to be announced, and she was standing
  on it. **There is no word within nine days of road for either of the two things she has learned this
  season: the noise, and the quiet.**
- **A stretch nobody is hired on** — the worst ground on nine days, on nobody's book, nobody's job — was
  **named by the sub-contractor in the ordinary way and she did not go on it and has not asked about it.**
  Past the ground she was standing on, and Batch 0004 begins on it.
- **Yuan.** Voice. **Four ration spends in fourteen chapters — ch 210, 213, 218, 222 — and two of them buy
  nothing at all, and the chapter does not say what he said on the fourth.** **No figure of what is left is
  stated anywhere in ch 209–222: no total, no arithmetic, no fraction, no rate.** The ration has been going
  down on its own for about three months and she is the only person in the world keeping a count of it.
  **He does not grow back and he is quieter at the end of this batch than at the start.** **Ledger: not
  ordered. The standing was used once, at ch 216, and not explained and not refused a second time.**
  **Merit: none. Nothing in ch 209–222 kills anything or clears a listed trial and the channel does not
  come back.** **The right index finger is named nowhere, priced nowhere and foreshadowed nowhere.** He
  **still cannot read the office name above this county and is silent about it throughout the batch.**
- **The crew at Qinghe** — **nine men and her; ten sleepers in twelve bunks, nine days west.** Not in this
  batch. Not mentioned, not raised, not counted.
- **Money.** About **ten coppers** in hand at the end of the batch. **Six coppers a day and a place at the
  fire at the course; five coppers at the sub-contractor's trestle.** The difference is never explained and
  is not a mystery. **The tin is gone since the evening of day 268** and nothing after that calls it
  anything but gone, and it is not counted anywhere in this batch.
- **Nothing Volume 3 left open was answered.** The claim stands with its date; Hou Sanliang is not met;
  what became of the eleven is not raised; the office above this county is not named and is not asked for;
  the sponsors' second book is lodged nowhere and spoken of nowhere; **Shen Xiu unforgiven and unthanked;
  the register still reads *reconciled*; the Clay Plate still worn; Ning Wu does not appear.** The ch 150
  refusal is not softened, deferred, reopened or regretted in any line, and no line lets her wonder what she
  would have done. **The year of non-forgiveness was not touched at all in this batch.**
  **She does not forgive him and she does not tell him to stop.**

---

## Volume 4 Batch 0004 second half, review repair (fix phase)

Repaired against `logs/next-0003.review.log` **without restarting the batch, without changing the planned
plot and without moving a pinned date.** **No title changed, no chapter order changed, no card rewritten,
no stage announced earlier or later, no cost, irreversible decision or ending changed, and nothing Volume 5
owns was spent.** Eight sentences changed inside prose that was already written — one of them a duplicated
paragraph cut — and every state figure that carried them was corrected with them. **Read this before using any ch 231–238 figure, date or place
quoted anywhere else in the state files.** The four pinned stage days (386, 412, 440, 462) and the pinned
attendance day (389) stand exactly as they were.

- **1 — A duplicated paragraph, and the only true mechanical fault in the eight chapters (ch 236).** *He
  turned and looked out along the ground the way a man looks out along ground he has walked every working
  day of nine years* appeared twice, once either side of *The line is not stone*. The second copy is gone.
  No sentence was rewritten and the beat still works: he looks out along the ground, says the line is not
  stone, and then says what it is.
- **2 — A date that could not exist, in the one place ch 234 had pinned it (ch 236).** She stands at the far
  board **on the twenty-second**, and the chapter put the blank strip *between a line dated the twenty-first
  and a line dated the second of the next* — **the second of the next month is day 465 and she is at day
  455.** It is now **between a line dated the thirtieth of the month before and a line dated about the
  ninth of this one**, which brackets the seventh, and ch 234's *On the seventh of that month he put one up*
  is what the strip is. The paragraph after it no longer claims she could not tell a reading from a
  non-reading among the flanking lines — **the flanking lines are readings, the strip between them is hers,
  and it is a hole the width of a stage.** `state/chapter-summaries.md` carried the broken pair and is
  corrected with it.
- **3 — The far-side board was in two places at once (ch 238).** Ch 236, ch 237 and ch 238 itself put it at
  **the post on the rise**, four hundred yards short of the trestle, and the final image said *behind her at
  the trestle*. It now reads **behind her, up the rise**. **The final image is otherwise untouched** — a
  wage-book open on a trestle, a column of hours that is hers, the price said out loud, and a board on a
  post with nine lines in it, eight written and one with nothing at all — exactly as
  `outline/volume-04.md` fixes it and as the phase prompt pinned it.
- **4 — The keeper's cadence contradicted itself between two chapters (ch 237).** Ch 236 gives **a strip
  about four inches wide every nine or ten days**, which is what ch 216 and ch 234 both establish; ch 237
  said he **writes one line a day and has written one a day for nine years**. Ch 237 now reads **puts one
  up every nine or ten days and has done for nine years whether or not he has anything to put in it**, and
  the scene is better for it: he comes up with the cart, stops with the pen an inch above the cloth for as
  long as it takes to read a line, and writes something he has no reason to write except that he heard it.
- **5 — A promise was dated to the wrong day (ch 235).** *I said on the seventh that I would come and look
  at it on the eleventh* — **the promise is made at ch 233 on the fifth, and ch 234's seventh has no such
  line in it.** Now *on the fifth*. The man still does not come, and nothing else in the beat moved.
- **6 — The money on the plank contradicted the wage the chapter had already fixed (ch 231).** *About five
  coppers in her hand on the twenty-fourth and about five on the thirtieth*, five in and five out for bread
  — and then *four coppers on the plank* on the thirtieth, in the same chapter. **Five.**
- **7 — A figure the ledger did not carry.** The blind run pays **two coppers a day in her hand and in no
  book at all** from the fifth (ch 233), which is on the page and was missing from the money rows. The
  *Hours* and *Money* rows of the ch 238 ledger now say so, and record that **no balance is stated anywhere
  in ch 231–238.**
- **8 — State files that had drifted from their own chapters.** Corrected in
  `state/chapter-summaries.md`: a **garbled run-together at the end of the ch 238 batch-position paragraph**
  (*stop.ure stated*), a **section header and a Volumes 5–16 line that still said the second half was not
  drafted**, and the ch 236 row's broken dates. In `state/book-metadata.md` and
  `workspace/volume-04-batch-0004/SUMMARY.md`: **the bracket count in Batch 0004 is THREE (ch 224, 228 and
  237), not four** — the sentence named three chapters and said four — and the word figures. Corrected in
  `state/current.md`: **two spends in ch 231–238, not three** (ch 231's mark and ch 233's standing are the
  only two the chapters show). In `state/continuity.md`: two blocks that still declared the second half
  **NOT DRAFTED** now point at the drafted record instead of contradicting it.
- **9 — The word figures, recounted off the files after the repairs.** Body prose excluding `#` and `##`
  heading lines: **ch 231–238 = 15,584 over eight chapters** (was 15,634), **Batch 0004 = 31,490 over
  sixteen, average ~1,968**, **Volume 4 file set = 120,566**. Longest ch 235 at 2,279, shortest ch 225 at
  1,672, **three chapters in the batch under the 1,800 guide (225, 233, 234) and ten in the volume**. The
  figures in `state/book-metadata.md`, `state/chapter-summaries.md`, `state/current.md` and the batch
  summary are all corrected to these.
- **Checked and passed, nothing changed.** Chapter markdown (no blockquote, code block, table or rule),
  every speech closed, the eight titles unique against all 238, **one bracketed layer line in eight chapters
  and it is at ch 237, ch 234 carrying none by order**, no calendar month and no weekday named anywhere in
  ch 231–238, no *eleven weeks*, no *about four seconds*, none of the retired constructions, no paragraph
  over 120 words (longest 113, in ch 230), and every sentence Yuan speaks in the present tense.
- **Carried to the close audit rather than rewritten.** In ch 237, *There are nine counties inside a line
  and one of them has just been read a figure out loud in a room that is not on their side of anything* is
  a passive construction that parses two ways, and the sentence after it is the one that carries the
  meaning: **the figure was read out, and the people in that room can act on it.** Nothing about the claim's
  standing was touched. **The board's nine lines and its nine years of strips** are both fixed by
  `outline/volume-04.md`'s final image and by ch 234, and were left exactly as written.

## Volume 4 Batch 0004 first half, review repair (fix phase)

Repaired against `logs/next-0002.review.log` **without restarting the batch, without changing the planned plot,
and without moving a pinned date.** **No title changed, no chapter order changed, no card rewritten, no stage
announced earlier or later, no cost, irreversible decision or ending changed. No chapter was rewritten: every
repair below is a sentence, a date, a distance or a figure inside the prose that was already written.** The
prose the reviewer passed is untouched. **The four pinned stage days (386, 412, 440, 462) and the pinned
attendance day (389) stand exactly as they were.** Read this before using any ch 223–230 date, distance or
figure quoted anywhere else in the state files.

- **1 — The batch's own engine had a date in it that would have collapsed it (ch 223).** The course's book
  was said to run *"from the fourth of the month before last to the seventeenth of this one"* — which, on the
  pinned calendar, put hours of the current month (day 390) into a book **whose partner the sub-contractor
  had been keeping since day 364**, so her hours would have sat in two hands across the same seventeen days
  and "two books and not one run in either" would have been false. **Now: *to the seventeenth of the month
  before*, which is day 360** — the day her name was struck, the day `outline/batches/volume-04-batch-0004.md`
  §The money already pins (*her name was struck out on the seventeenth and the hours to that day stand*),
  and the day ch 221 and ch 224's Cui Bao already name. The hole between the two books is three days and is
  true.
- **2 — The seventh stage was a day late, which moved the base's turn (ch 228, and its echo in ch 229).**
  Foundation 7 is **pinned to day 412, the ninth**, and the chapter took it *"on the second of the three
  days"* = day 413. **The stage section has been moved ahead of the working section and retaken on the ninth
  at the fourth hour**, which also puts it before the two hours she spends working out why the ground is
  quiet instead of after them; no prose was cut, the night paragraph moved into the first day and reads *the
  dark of the first night*, and ch 229's *"the second of the three days"* became *the first*. **Twenty-eight
  days from day 412 is day 440, the suppressed eighth at ch 234**, so ch 230's arithmetic now stands.
- **3 — Two figures in ch 230 that the next phase would have inherited.** *"The fifth of the next month,
  which is eighteen days off"* → **thirteen days off** (day 438 is thirteen days after the twenty-second, and
  it is the day ch 233 is pinned to). The ration line's *"do not last the fortnight that is in front of her"*
  → **as far as the day that is in front of her**. The error came from `outline/batches/volume-04-batch-0004.md`
  *Batch midpoint*, which said *eighteen days short of the suppressed eighth* and *ten days past Foundation 7*;
  the card now reads **fourteen days short and eight days past**. **Neither the card nor any chapter may put
  ch 233 on a day other than 438–439.**
- **4 — Ch 225's offered word contradicted the scene it stood in.** The chapter recorded Pei as having
  offered her the word *in the same paragraph that has him refusing to fill the column*, and said in another
  paragraph that nobody had offered it a second time. **One construction now runs through all three:** two
  men, one offer each, never a second time from either — **the decent man at a trestle inside the wire, and
  Pei himself on an earlier round of his**, which is what his *"I have said that to you before"* means. Her
  **"Twice."** and his **"Twice."** are the lines that beat depends on and both stand. The state files are
  corrected to match; `state/current.md` and `state/continuity.md` no longer claim she **said the reason
  aloud**, which she does not do anywhere in ch 223–230 — the reason is narration, once, and not in her mouth.
  **That matters because ch 231 is pinned to the price being stated in the ordinary voice, once, and a state
  file that says she already said a reason aloud in this shape spends the next chapter's construction.**
- **5 — Ch 225 ran its clock backwards.** Ch 224 closes at the seventh hour of the fourteenth; ch 225 opened
  with the carrier arriving *on the fourteenth at about the second hour*. **The carrier now comes on the
  fifteenth** and the sheet is written on the sixteenth, which is what the heading *The Sixteenth* always
  said. **Ch 225 as written runs 388–393, not 387–393** — recorded below and in the batch summary as a
  second deliberate deviation from the card's day row. Pei also no longer reads the entry out as *"Member
  absent"* before the day he writes it, and *two days before* the sixth stage became *three days before*.
- **6 — Three distances and two objects in one chapter (ch 224, ch 228).** *"There were about two hundred
  yards of ground between the trestle and where he stopped, and he stopped at the trestle"* cancelled itself;
  the term is **the wire**, and Cui Bao crosses the two hundred yards and stops at the trestle. **The
  course is nine hundred yards inside the wire and the wire is two hundred yards from the trestle**, which is
  the figure the rest of the volume uses, and `state/chapter-summaries.md` no longer says the course is two
  hundred yards in. The tender's hand is now on **the props the board stands on** (ch 216: *two planks set
  on end props*) instead of on the sub-contractor's trestle, and in ch 228 the foreman's board **stands at
  the wire on the course's own ground** and is not called the trestle, which belongs to the sub-contractor
  past the last stake. Cui Bao's *"the eighth"* is now *the eighth of the month before*, which is day 351 and
  not day 381.
- **7 — The money drifted against a wage the book fixes as flat (ch 223, 226, 228).** Five in the morning and
  five out in the evening and the number does not move. **Ch 228's *about three coppers* is now *about five***,
  which is also what ch 229 gives for the twelfth, and ch 226's balance sentence now says **five in the
  morning and five at the bread counter in the evening** instead of reading as five both ways. **The bread is
  five coppers on every page**: ch 226's *"gave the woman four coppers for it"* is five, and the four coppers
  in the shop belongs to **the oil**, which is now named as the oil. **Ch 230's *about ten coppers* is
  correct and was left alone** — it is the fourth hour on the twenty-second, before that day's bread — and
  the ledger line in `state/continuity.md` has been corrected to the true pair of figures instead.
- **8 — Three backward month references did not reconcile with the calendar.** The tin is gone on **day 268**,
  which is the fifteenth of a month four months before ch 226 and five before ch 229; **ch 226's *five* is now
  *four* and ch 229's *five* stands.** **Ch 225's carrier — ch 210, day 286 — is *three months back*, not
  four.** **Ch 229's *fourth of a month two months back* is *three months back***, which is the month ch 223
  dates the loss of the order to. **Deliberately left alone:** ch 223's *"the eighteenth of a month two months
  back"* and the *twenty-six days* that follow it, which do not reconcile to the pinned day 338 and would
  need a sentence rewritten rather than a figure corrected, and ch 224's unattached *"three months back"*,
  which names no event.
- **9 — The ration's own arithmetic did not reconcile, and the state files counted it wrong.** Ch 223 spends a
  mark on the tenth of the month — that spend is carded, it is on the page, and it is why the count is four
  marks in ch 228 and ch 230. **Ch 228's *had not spent one of them in five days* and ch 230's *She had not
  spent one in five days* are now *since the tenth of the month before*** (day 383 to day 412 is twenty-nine
  days, not five), **and ch 230's *she has not added to it since the tenth of a month that is now four
  months back* is gone** — the last spend before the twenty-second is the tenth of the month before, and the
  four marks have not moved since. **There are two ration spends in ch 223–230, not one:** ch 223, which
  bought nothing at all, and ch 230, which bought four sentences. Corrected in `state/continuity.md`,
  `state/current.md`, `state/open-threads.md`, the batch summary and the next phase's prompt.
- **10 — State files that had silently corrected their own chapters (finding 8 of the review).** Corrected:
  `state/chapter-summaries.md` **five feet → two feet** (ch 223), **two hundred → nine hundred yards**
  (ch 224), **ten coppers → about five** and **the fourth stage's cost → the sixth stage's cost** (ch 225),
  **the second of the three days → the first** and **the board's location** (ch 228), **three months → two
  months back** (ch 230). Corrected in `state/continuity.md` and `state/current.md`: the offered-word
  construction, the reason-not-spoken, the money ledger and the ch 225 day span. Corrected in
  `workspace/continuation/next-0003/PROMPT.md`: **eighteen days → thirteen days off**, so the next phase
  cannot inherit it.
- **11 — A ten-word overcount, and the figures that carried it.** The half was recorded at 15,884 words. Body
  prose excluding `#` and `##` heading lines is **15,906 over eight chapters, average ~1,988** — longest ch 228
  at 2,244, shortest ch 225 at 1,672, **still one chapter under the 1,800 guide (225)**, forty-nine section
  headings, longest paragraph 113 words. Corrected in `state/book-metadata.md`,
  `state/chapter-summaries.md`, `state/open-threads.md`, `state/current.md` and the batch summary. **Nothing

## Volume 4 Batch 0004 planning review repair (fix phase)

Repaired against `logs/volume-04-batch-0004.review.log` **without restarting the phase and without changing the
planned plot.** **No card was rewritten, no card was added, dropped, merged or renumbered, no day moved, no
stage moved, no cost, irreversible decision or ending touched.** Batch 0004 is still a planning turn: **sixteen
cards exist and no chapter is drafted.** Every fix below is a correction to a *number, a rule or a citation* in
a planning file. **Read this before using any batch 0004 figure or date quoted anywhere else in the state files.**

- **1 — The chapter count. Batch 0004 is sixteen chapters, not fourteen.** 223–238 inclusive is sixteen, the
  allocation table in `outline/volume-04.md` *Pressure rotation across the volume* gives **0004 = ch 223–238**,
  and that table's four rows (14 + 16 + 14 + 16) sum to the volume's sixty. The phase prompt said **fourteen**
  in five places — *Fourteen cards* twice, *fourteen cards* twice, *fourteen titles* once — and the card file
  inherited it while writing **sixteen** cards. Corrected in
  `workspace/volume-04-batch-0004/PROMPT.md` (five places) and in `outline/batches/volume-04-batch-0004.md` (five:
  the batch goal, *Chapter range*, *Required power changes*, and the two bracket-count lines — **thirteen of the
  sixteen chapters** carry no bracket, not eleven of fourteen). **Three "fourteen" figures are correct and were
  deliberately left alone**: Batch 0003's own fourteen chapters (ch 209–222), ch 235's fourteen days on the
  blind run, and Batch 0003's fourteen chapters carrying quoted speech.
- **2 — A note at the foot of the card file cited a heading that does not exist.** It attributed a conflicting
  chapter count to `outline/volume-04.md` ***What changed***. **That section is not in the file.** The claim has
  been replaced with a true record: the figure came from the phase prompt, sixteen is correct, and the card file
  now says sixteen throughout.
- **3 — A stale word count in this file.** Line 22 reported Batch 0003 as **28,068 / ~2,005** while labelling the
  figures post-review-repair. **Those were the pre-repair numbers**, and they disagreed with
  `state/book-metadata.md`, `state/continuity.md`, `state/chapter-summaries.md`, `state/open-threads.md` and the
  phase prompt, which all carry **28,568 / ~2,041**. Corrected here. **Recounted off the files to confirm:**
  body prose only, excluding `#` and `##` heading lines, ch 209–222 = **28,568 words over fourteen chapters,
  average 2,041, longest 218 (2,425), shortest 214 (1,666)** — exactly the post-repair figures, and the
  long-paragraph and heading counts of 99 words and 90 also check out.
- **4 — The calendar arithmetic in this file was wrong and the drafter would have inherited it.** It read
  *month-day = day − 133*. **It is now the rule and not an offset: months are thirty days, a month begins every
  thirty days, and the month-day of any day is `((day − 134) mod 30) + 1`,** because day 134 was the first of a
  month. **133 held only for day 134 to day 163 and holds nowhere in Volume 4.** **Verified against every date on
  the page**: day 136 (ch 150) the third · day 147 the fourteenth · day 178 the fifteenth · **day 376 (ch 222)
  the third** · day 386 the thirteenth · day 389 the sixteenth · day 404 the first · day 412 the ninth · day 462
  the twenty-ninth · day 470 the seventh, and **all twenty-nine rows of the card file's day table reconcile**.
  Corrected in `state/current.md`, `state/open-threads.md`, `outline/batches/volume-04-batch-0004.md` (clock block
  and the day-table note) and `workspace/volume-04-batch-0004/PROMPT.md`. **Five more instances stood in
  `state/continuity.md`**, in the *Established facts* blocks for Batches 0001, 0002 and 0003 — the standing canon
  record the Batch 0004 drafting phase is told to read for canon, so the wrong label was corrected in all five,
  and **every derived date in them was checked and every one is already correct**: day 147 the fourteenth, day 178
  the fifteenth, day 239 the sixteenth, day 254 the first, day 281 the 28th, day 283 the 30th, day 284 the first,
  day 212 the nineteenth, day 213 the twentieth. **One of them also carried a broken derivation and is now fixed:
  the ch 239 line read *239 − 133 = 106, and 106 leaves 16 over three whole months*; the subtraction and the 106
  were wrong and day 239 is the sixteenth directly.** **Deliberately not changed:** the same label still stands
  in the three closed batches' own card files (`outline/batches/volume-04-batch-0001/0002/0003.md`) and in this
  file's historical Volume 3 audit table. Their derived dates are correct, they are closed artifacts, and Batch
  0004 is pinned by its own card file, which carries the correct rule — **reopening closed batches to relabel a
  formula would cost more than it is worth, and a drafter is not sent to those files for facts.**
- **5 — One figure found while applying the fixes and corrected here.** `state/book-metadata.md` and the phase
  prompt said **three** chapters of Batch 0003 sat below the 1,800 word guide. **One does: ch 214 at 1,666.**
  Ch 212 (1,809) and ch 219 (1,856) clear the guide. Both files now say so.
- **6 — Two items the reviewer logged as decisions and NOT repairs, now carried to the volume close.**
  **The point of view**, and **the close phase**. Neither is touched by this repair and **nothing in ch 223–238
  may settle either.** `outline/series.md` §2 names **Yuan, male**, as the protagonist and the metadata records
  Gender: Male, so the male-led requirement holds at series level, but **Volume 4 is close third on Ning Yan and
  Yuan has been a voice only, and absent from the page's point of view, for two volumes of sixteen.** That is

- **Nothing was found wrong with the chapters.** The reviewer's mechanical pass over ch 209–222 was clean on

## Volume 4 Batch 0003 review repair (fix phase)

Repaired against `logs/volume-04-batch-0003.review.log` **without restarting the batch and without changing the
planned plot.** No title changed, no chapter order changed, no card moved, no day moved, no stage announced
earlier or later, no cost, no irreversible decision and no ending changed. **Read this before using any batch
0003 figure quoted anywhere else in the state files.**

- **Four hard errors, all small line repairs.** **ch 221:** a garbled clause, *"in the two weeks that
  followedowing two weeks"* → *"in the two weeks that followed."* **ch 209:** a crew of eleven men and a girl
  was sending forty-five men to cells — **"about forty of them… and five went to one"** → **"about nine of
  them… and two went to one."** **ch 217:** *"she had done it four times in her life — once at eleven, once at
  fifteen, once at nineteen"* put one of the four in her future at an age she never reaches; **she had done it
  twice in her life, and this was the one that finished.** **ch 219:** the only calendar month in the batch,
  *"the hand is the thing that went in June"* → **"…that went on the twenty-fifth"**, which is the day ch 217
  gives. **No calendar month is named anywhere in ch 209–222.**
- **The only standing fact the review changed: she has rebuilt the order in her hand twice, not four times.**
  The heel went once, three years and a bit ago; she put two years into rebuilding; Foundation 4 took it again.
  Everything else in the ch 217 cost — the four inches, the turn that cannot be built, the writing hand
  unchanged — is as drafted.
- **Five of fourteen chapters carried no dialogue at all** (212, 214, 217, 219, 220) and the batch had drifted
  toward narration *about* a scene. **All fourteen now carry quoted speech.** Every added exchange is ordinary
  camp speech and **changes no fact, no number, no card and no ending**: three men at the trestle arguing about
  the thirty-nine while she waits for a sentence that does not come (212) · the girl of nineteen naming that
  she is stopping on every pull (214) · a man asking why she stops, and being told her hand is slow (217) · a
  man asking the foreman whether the stretch comes back, and the girl asking whether the forty will be put on
  (219) · **the foreman's own daughter asking him in the open what the line through the name is, and being
  told once and flat that the hours are in the book and stay in it (220).**
- **Restated insights cut** where ch 213 delivered "she found it / she cannot prove it / she told nobody" across
  three closing blocks, and where ch 212 restated the floor she built out of nine seconds of a decent man.
- **Section-heading density reduced**: twenty-six argument labels deleted, **116 headings down to 90, 8.3 a
  chapter down to 6.4**, against Volume 3's 6.2 and Volume 2's 2.3. Scene divisions all stay.
- **Corrected figures.** **28,568 words, average ~2,041** (was 28,068 / ~2,005). Longest ch 218 (2,425),
  shortest **ch 214 (1,666)**. Below the 1,800 guide: **ch 214 only (1,666)** — 212 (1,809) and 219 (1,856)
  clear it by nine and fifty-six words and an earlier draft of this line wrongly listed all three as below.
  **Longest paragraph is 99 words (ch 220)** — the summary had said 94 — and none is over 120; median paragraph 31
  words.
- **Known trade-off, stated rather than hidden.** The batch-level monotony the reviewer also logged — *"That
  is"* opening 86 sentences in 1,146, against *"She had"* 61 and *"It is"* 50 — was **not** swept across all
  fourteen chapters. A blanket rewrite of the declarative gear would have cost the good prose the reviewer
  passed and the batch its voice. It was reduced where it was thickest (ch 212 and ch 213) and left alone
  where it is doing the work of the present tense the novel runs on. **There are still zero same-opener
  repeats back to back within a paragraph**, so this is monotony, not a mechanical breach, and it is the
  right thing for the next audit to take up rather than this one.

## Who is where at end of chapter 208 — VOLUME 4 BATCH 0002 CLOSED (cold reader starts here)

Full record: `state/continuity.md` under ***Established facts, Volume 4 Batch 0002 (ch 193–208)*** and
***Volume 4 running ledger — at end of ch 208***. **Day 280.** She is on the course, in a bunk with three
boards and a mat on it, nine days east of Lowwater, with about ten coppers and four stubs of paper in an
oilcloth.

- **Ning Yan** — **FOUNDATION 1, reached at day 276 and announced at her own order in the ordinary way,
  and heard by nobody.** Ceiling at **Foundation the first stage**, relicensed on the **second** set of
  legs, all three of them outside this county: **a contractor's clearance at a counter on day 264**, **three
  marks on a sheet in a room at the west end of the camp on the evening of day 265**, and **a countersignature
  in an office's hand and not a person's on day 266**, applied to a schedule made by **Ren Shu** reading the
  contractor's wage-book aloud to a room she was not in the middle of. **The relicensed licence is a document
  she has never touched.** Seven conditions referenced, never re-listed; the claim the seventh and last, on
  the back, untouched. **The stage column reads one, two, three, five, six, seven, nine, one** — three holes
  in nine lines as before, **in the county books of four counties out of nine and read out across all nine.**
  **Nine counties have heard nothing this year**; the four books that would carry it are nine days west and
  have not been told. Short breath three counts on stairs, four on a grade, never back to two. **The heel of
  her left hand is gone** and every county hour still takes eleven minutes with the flat of a second finger.
  Right arm open a third of the time. Aperture cracked through and permanent. **A cold has started under her
  ribs that will not go for four weeks** — a body that has never been full and cold at the same time — and
  **she is not hungry.**
- **MEMBER ABSENT.** **This is the batch's irreversible decision and the volume's. Day 269, the sixteenth.**
  Entered in the county's own two words, in the hand that writes *Member present*, in the attendance column
  of the county's monthly member sheet, which travels with the work. **Beside it, in a column that is not
  joined to it, nine days of her hours stand in order with dates on them in a contractor's hand, and every
  one of them is good, and no quantity of hours reaches the attendance.** **She was offered the one word
  that could have gone in the reason column, by Ma Sheng, unasked, in the ordinary voice, and did not write
  it** — because **the reason column is the column her hours go in**, in the only book in the world that can
  certify an hour she worked out here, read in order with dates by an office past the boundary. **Permanent.
  Not payable in hours. On her member's sheet. The first thing she has ever lost by choosing to move rather
  than by being stopped**, and she was not stopped: she did not count the sixteenth, and there are four days
  in the road where she could have turned round, and she has refused out loud in her own head the version in
  which anything was taken out of her hands.
- **The wage-book.** A contractor's book with four columns — **date · stretch · hours · price** — and the
  price column is the reason column, and the hours go in it. Eleven men, a foreman, a girl of nineteen, a
  tender, **paid by the day at six coppers a day and a place at the fire**, which does not leave anything to
  put by. **Six coppers is the day's wage at the end of the road and the passage to reach it cost more per
  day than the wage pays.**
- **The first honest description of the line is on the page, at ch 199, in a worker's mouth.** They call it
  **the line**, call the work **tending**, call the job **the course**. Where the work is done well and on
  time the line holds; where it is not, it leaks, and what comes out of a badly kept stretch is **drift**, and
  the drift is thicker where the work is worst. Ninety parts of it: the residue of everything said inside
  goes in the ground on the far side and runs along and pools and thickens where the ground is soft. **They
  are right and nothing in this volume proves them wrong.** What the drift is made of and who put it there is
  not answered and was not asked.
- **Cui Bao**, about fifty, foreman, eleven men, **a daughter of nineteen on the line**, right about
  everything and wanting nothing from her but her hours. **Ma Sheng**, about thirty, tender, **nine years on
  the course**, keeps the carriers' board, read the west-going line out of it for her in under a minute for
  nothing, and brought her a second blanket without a word. **Neither is a villain and neither is wrong.**
- **Ren Shu**, a clerk of the schedule office at Stony Ford, who reads a contractor's book aloud to a room
  and not to her. **The office that countersigned is not his office and was not named.**
- **Luo Ping** — **found, and at work.** Thirty-nine, sixteen years of other people's schedules, four or five
  offices in a season. **He told her, unasked, exactly what his trade is, and that nobody in sixteen years
  has ever asked him whether a date ought to have gone in. He does not know who sent him on that occasion
  and would not say if he did.** **Finding him answered nothing.** The claim stands, the date is not torn
  off, **Hou Sanliang is unpaid, patient, and has never been met and does not appear.** His wife and boy of
  eleven are **eleven days west** and she did not tell him she knew, because it would be a way of making him
  small.
- **Bai Zhu.** At ch 196 only, on high ground, in a plain coat with a notebook, having walked east ahead of a
  road he is under no instruction to walk. **He bought nothing, threatened nothing, touched her, entered her
  rooms, and did not ask her a question and said so in the same breath.** He gave her one true thing about
  where she was going that he had no business giving, in the ordinary voice, **and she did not thank him,
  because thanking him would have made his work into a kindness.** **Named in nobody's mouth; no document
  he signs was produced; he is not seen again in this batch.** The referral and the request are **still
  pending** and the district's answer is **still four words and an empty space the width of a line.**
- **The counterfoils.** **Four stubs of paper off a contractor's book, about nine inches by two**, each with
  a date, hours, stretch, rate and the contractor's mark, and a painted mark of the stretch on the corner.
  **This is the only object in the world that can certify an hour she worked out there.** She keeps them in
  the oilcloth, above the Clay Plate, with three dead weight tickets.
- **She has seen three hundred yards of a nine-day line.** The map is on the back of a board in a room and
  **nine days** is written on it. Cui Bao has not walked it end to end this season and most men have not.
- **Yuan.** Voice. **About two days of channel at ch 192, rationed, thinning** — **spent once in this batch
  at ch 193 and wasted, once at ch 194, once at ch 196 and it bought the truest thing he has said to her in
  a volume, two at ch 203 and they bought the least, and once at ch 204.** **At the worst of the second day
  on the climb her hand was on it and she did not order it, and no price is named anywhere in ch 193–208.**
  **Ledger: not ordered, no refusal scene, no price.** **Merit: none. Nothing in this batch kills anything or
  clears anything the layer has listed.** **He does not grow back and he is quieter at the end of this batch
  than at the start of it.** He is **silent throughout about the office above this county.**
- **The crew at Qinghe** — **nine men and her; ten sleepers in twelve bunks.** She has not seen them since
  the morning of the first of the month, **cannot send anything and cannot tell them anything**, and named
  them in her own head at ch 205 as the cost. **Gao kept the second bunk and said so instead of saying the
  sum a third time. Qin walked her to the dressed stone and put a warm flat of bread into her hand and asked
  nothing.** Nine men are nine days west and have not been told anything.
- **The girl of twenty-four** — alive, forty-two copied run lists, **the forty-second carried the course's
  roll of hands and is the only reason a roster has ever reached a wash house in this county.** Not thanked,
  not paid, not offered.
- **Money** — **about thirty coppers on day 254**, spent on a seat at a contractor's cart at the fourth
  bridge (twenty-two coppers, two days, to the turn of the Verge) and two days of food and four more days of
  bread. **Six coppers a day in and six coppers a day out.** **The tin is gone by the evening of day 268**
  and nothing after that calls it anything but gone.
- **Nothing Volume 3 left open was answered.** The empty space is still empty; the claim stands with its
  date; what became of the eleven is not raised; the office above this county is not named; the sponsors'
  second book is lodged nowhere and spoken of nowhere; **Shen Xiu unforgiven and unthanked; the register
  still reads *reconciled*; the Clay Plate still worn; Feng Lushi alive and not thanked; Ning Wu does not
  appear.** **She does not forgive him and she does not tell him to stop**, and the year was touched once at
  ch 206, at low volume, as a purchase that is still carrying with not one line in it that says settled.
- **Nothing is announced in this country.** It is not a failure. There is a room and she is not in it.

## Who is where at end of chapter 192 — VOLUME 4 BATCH 0001 CLOSED (cold reader starts here)

Full record: `state/continuity.md` under ***Established facts, Volume 4 Batch 0001 (ch 179–192)*** and
***Volume 4 running ledger — at end of ch 192***. **Day 253.** The season's road reopens on **day 254**.

- **Ning Yan** — **CONDENSING 9.** Unchanged all fourteen chapters; **no stage was taken and no stage was
  prepared.** Ceiling 9 in her own hand, relicensed ch 132, **seven conditions on the back, referenced and
  never re-listed; the claim the seventh and last thing on the paper. There is nothing above nine on the
  front of the document anywhere in the world.** The stage column reads **one, two, three, five, six, seven,
  nine — three holes in nine lines, in the county books of four counties out of nine and read out across all
  nine.** Short breath three counts on stairs, never back to two. **The heel of her left hand is gone** and
  every county hour takes eleven minutes with the flat of a second finger. Right arm open a third of the
  time. Aperture cracked through and permanent. **The second licence is nailed STRAIGHT above the second
  bunk; the first is rolled in the lining of her coat.** The Foundation petition is **entered, numbered,
  routed, barred — not refused — and not withdrawable by her.**
- **Yuan** — Voice. **About two days of channel left**, rationed at **four sentences a day**, and the ration
  is thinning on its own. **Ledger was taken once in this batch, at ch 187, for eight days out of about ten,
  named aloud before the word, held under a day, put down when it was over and not offered to hold it again**
  — and **refused once out loud at ch 191**, on a mat, before he could say the price, with no explanation
  attached. **Merit: none. Nothing in ch 179–192 can restore the channel.** He still cannot read the office
  name above this county and **did not say so a fifth time in this batch.** Four conditions hers, three his,
  standing and never re-listed.
- **Luo Ping — NEW MINOR, ch 187.** Thirty-nine, a **schedule-man**: a working man who keeps a schedule for
  somebody else's office and is sent in to put dates into it, four or five offices in a season, sixteen
  years. **He wrote the date against the claim into Hou Sanliang's counting room in Lowwater by a hand that
  is not that room's.** He has a wife and a boy of eleven **past this county's boundary.** **She has not
  gone to find him, has not written to him, and does not appear in a room in this batch.**
- **The referral** — up the stair past every office she has ever stood in front of, carrying her name, her
  stages in order and her hours, carrying no agent's name, executable only past a boundary no county paper
  crosses. **The district's answer on the public shelf is still four words and an empty space the width of
  a line. It was read at ch 179 and referred to at ch 189 and it is still empty at ch 192.**
- **The claim** — eight hundred jin, standing, unanswered, **the date still in the counting room and not torn
  off**. **Hou Sanliang unpaid, patient, never in a room with her, never met.**
- **The girl of twenty-four** — alive, a crooked leg, four paid hours a week at the trough plus four more out
  of a district fund for the cold months, a quarter paid forward to the thaw, **not thanked**. **She is now
  the holder of forty-one copied carriers' run lists**, which are the source of everything known about what
  the carriers say about the pass and about the point past which they do not run.
- **The crew** — **nine men and her; ten sleepers in twelve bunks.** They have read the refusal, the nine, the
  word on the front of their own schedule and three holes in a column. **Gao holds the frame and has now said
  the sum out loud twice**, the second time in front of her. **Qin now stands beside her in a queue instead of
  eight feet off**, and on the second morning the stone stopped he **noticed the fire had gone out, filled a
  bucket himself, and asked nothing.** The word *irregular* is on the front of the quarterly for the fourth
  time in a year and **is not stamped a fifth.**
- **Da Shan** — the board behind a well, eight frames, **nine coppers a load, four loads a week**. **He lost
  those four loads a week on the morning she stopped buying and said the true thing about it and was not
  thanked.** He has never asked her a question and never will.
- **Feng Lushi** — alive, warm, column at five, a Listener for life, a quarter off his own stair at his own
  written request, **has not asked her a question since the autumn and is not thanked.** He told her, in
  ch 181, why he will not.
- **Shen Xiu** unforgiven, unthanked, **does not appear**. **The three sponsors silent** and nothing about
  their second book is lodged, copied or spoken in ch 179–192. **The register still reads *reconciled*. The
  Clay Plate is still worn.** Ning Wu does not appear.
- **Money** — **about thirty coppers** under a board in the corner of the wash house where the water does
  not get. **The tin is not empty and is not spent**; it is being held for a road.
- **The decision** — **she is going east when the road opens, on purpose, with him.** Not to escape the hunt,
  which is behind her and out of reach. Because **no office inside this county will certify hours worked
  past its boundary**, and the name she bought points that way, and a second relicensing needs a schedule.
  **She has not packed, has not said goodbye, and has not told nine men in a speech.**
- **She does not forgive him and she does not tell him to stop.** The ch 150 refusal is not softened,
  deferred, reopened or regretted anywhere in ch 179–192, and no chapter lets her wonder what she would have
  done. **The year of non-forgiveness changed shape at ch 185 and ch 188 and did not close:** the sentence
  she kept back once a year went on a price instead, and what replaced the refusal is a purchase.

## Who is where at end of chapter 178 — VOLUME 3 CLOSED AND AUDITED (Volume 4's inherited position; still binding)

- **Ning Yan** — **CONDENSING 9. Announced at her own order on day 198, in the open, at the cold end, in
  front of nine men.** Ceiling 9, relicensed ch 132, **seven conditions on the back, referenced and never
  re-listed; the claim is the seventh and the last thing on the paper.** **There is nothing above nine on
  the front of the document anywhere in the world**, and a registrar has said so out loud in a room.
  **The stage column reads one, two, three, five, six, seven, nine — three holes in nine lines, written into
  the county books of four counties out of nine, and read out across all nine.** **Nine counties is what is
  heard; four counties is what is written** (V1 ch 46, *in four counties of nine*). **The fourth stage of her
  life and the eighth are on no paper in nine counties and never will be.** **Short breath three counts on stairs, never back to two. The heel of her
  left hand is gone** — she writes the county hour with the flat of a second finger, eleven minutes longer
  than she used to, **and she told nine men so out loud at ch 172.** Right arm open a third of the time.
  **The second licence is nailed STRAIGHT above the second bunk. The first is rolled in the lining of her
  coat**, taken down before it went soft at the crease of a defect line she wrote herself.
  **A relicensing with the ceiling at Foundation the first stage is petitioned, numbered, received, routed
  and BARRED — not refused — and cannot be withdrawn by her** (ch 175). **She does not forgive him. She
  does not tell him to stop.**
- **Yuan** — Voice. **Ledger was taken once in Volume 3, at ch 149, on her spoken word, in public, for under
  a day, at a price of four and a half weeks out of about ten that she named before she said the word; he
  put it down the same day and refuses permanently for the volume. From ch 150 Ledger is hers to order and
  his to hold only on her word, and at ch 178 she has it to order and does not order it.**
  **Channel about ten days (ch 178), said once, in weeks, in the final image, against about six weeks of
  winter still to come in it.** **He still cannot read the office name above this county and told her so in
  one breath.** Four conditions hers, three his. He sees her arm go before she can, at no cost, as standing
  Voice.
- **The referral** — **filed day 212, read twice in a low room in front of twenty people, and gone up the
  stair past every office she has ever stood in front of.** It carries her name, her stages **in order** and
  her hours. **It carries no agent's name.** The dismantling it orders can only be executed **past a
  boundary no county paper crosses**, for want of a Wall writ this county does not hold.
- **The girl of twenty-four** — **alive** at the wash house, leg set crooked, four paid hours a week at the
  trough **plus four more out of a district fund for the cold months**, because she wrote the second
  document and not the first. Paid a quarter to the thaw in advance. **Not thanked.** She has told Ning Yan
  out loud that a question of Ning Yan's put her under the line.
- **Qin** — **NEW MINOR, ch 169.** Twenty-two, two winters on the crew. Stood at the cold end for a shift
  and did not work, and she did not send him back. Walks four miles down the mountain to stand eight feet
  off in a queue while she writes a column. **Never asks her a question.**
- **Bo** — the quarry foreman, about fifty, three miles east on the Verge road. **The face is shut; the cut
  ran out on day 189.** **Kan** — the hoist-man, about forty, **forty-three cards written, her hour on
  thirty-four of them**, and he got her off a lip backwards in one movement. Both minor.
- **Da Shan** — the man at the board behind a well. **Eight frames, two of which had come off and are back.**
  Charging nine coppers a load and saying so to her face. Never asked a question and never will.
- **Feng Lushi** — **alive, warm for the first time in twenty-two years, coughing less, writing more,
  owned. Column at five.** A **Listener** for life. Suspended from the stair for one quarter **by his own
  written request**, a district clerk carrying the bundle upward and coming down empty. **Cannot read what
  is in the gaps of her stage column and has not been able to for nine years.** **Has not asked her a
  question since ch 143. Not thanked.**
- **Bai Zhu, the Hunter** — **named at ch 150 and on no document he signs.** Room over the tanner's under
  **Chou Zhyan**. One certified copy and a receipt in eleven weeks. **He has now certified in a Warden's
  hand that the System is real, unauthorised and carried by a licensee in good standing, and referred the
  dismantling upward.** Never lies, threatens, touches her, enters her rooms, or pays for force. **He has
  never asked her a question and has said so in one breath so that she does not have to spend a morning on
  it.** The request for the instrument he does not hold went live on the same crate as the referral.
- **Registrar Sun He** — nineteen years at the county book. **He entered the ninth, ruled three gaps with
  four words, wrote *Member present*, and stopped.** He bars her Foundation application in the ordinary hand
  on the ground that this county cannot certify a schedule of work past its boundary, with a last line
  written slower than the rest.
- **Hou Sanliang** — creditor on paper. **Unpaid, patient, never in a room with her, never met.** **A date
  has been entered against the claim in his counting room, in a hand that is not the counting room's.** The
  ch 150 handover would have paid him in full and she refused it, and at ch 174 she checks, standing in the
  room, that she does not wish she had said yes.
- **Shen Xiu** — unforgiven, unthanked. **Does not appear.** **The three sponsors** — do not appear; their
  purchased silence holds; **nothing about the second book is lodged, copied or spoken anywhere in
  ch 119–178.** **Mei Jiu, Ning Wu** — do not appear. The register still reads *reconciled*.
- **Dead this volume:** a bounty man in good boots on the escort road below the fourth bridge (ch 140),
  entered by her hand in the Watch's record. Du Hong, Cao Lei and Xu An remain in the ground from
  Volume 2. **Nothing else died in Volume 3 and nothing was rescued.**
- **Money:** the tin is **about a hundred and forty coppers**, under a board in the corner of the wash
  house where the water does not get. Stone at nine coppers a load, four loads a week, bought out of it.

## What Volume 3 must not do (cold reader reads this second)

Written after batch 0009 closed Volume 2; re-checked after Batches 0001, 0002 and 0003, and again at the
**Volume 3 close audit**, whose full table is below this block. **Everything here is now history that Volume 4 must respect.**

**Do not reopen the Watch, the burial line, the county debt line or the no-surety request.** All four are
lodged, stamped and closed on the page.

**Do not put her back on the fifth hour at the intake table.** The line moved to the ninth at ch 95 and
stands there. **Do not audit the change.**

**Do not spend a second strike request or a second outside-bound office.** The slip at ch 110 is the only one
in this run, and the volumes on the high shelf are not copies and were not struck.

**Do not answer what became of the eleven.** Deliberately unexplained for the whole run; Volume 12 owns it.

**The naming is spent.** The stranger at the first desk is unnamed through ch 149, **named once, at ch 150**,
by himself, to a clerk taking the agent's name off a form, and **never on a document he signs** — including
the certification and the referral at ch 174, both of which carry the office and not a person.

**The licence is at its top.** Ceiling nine in her own hand, **nothing above it on the document**, seven
conditions never re-listed, the claim the seventh. **Nothing above Foundation is licensed without a second
relicensing, and that is petitioned, barred and not withdrawable (ch 175); only an office above this county
can complete it, and only past a boundary no county paper crosses.** Foundation 1 is Volume 4.

**Do not break the silence bought at ch 128.** Nothing about the sponsors' second book — the nine struck
households, the eleven-year spacing, the fourth line citing the maintaining members' ledger, the roundhouse
hand in the frame of twelve unsigned sheets — is lodged, copied or spoken anywhere in ch 119–178. **The seal
was bought with silence. Confirmed true across all four batches.**

**Do not re-run the suppression arithmetic.** It is spent three times: ch 104, ch 159–160 and ch 161. At the
reading, Sun He rules the three gaps with four words and stops. **A suppressed stage leaves the same width of
absence as any other hole; say it once and do not argue it again.**

**The audit and the Lung are spent.** Audit ch 136–140, offer ch 141, taking ch 143. He is a **Listener**,
alive, warm, coughing less, column at five, suspended from the stair for one quarter **at his own written
request** and **not dismissed**. **Do not offer him a second appointment or a second Lung before Volume 11.**

**Do not open a second accident, death, frostbite, starvation or rescue.** **Volume 3 killed one man, at
ch 140, and rescued nobody.** The lip at ch 165 was the batch's one piece of physical risk and nobody was
hurt.

**Do not record a chapter-length trade-off.** Guide 1,800–2,700; floor 1,300. **Volume 3 closed at 135,014
words over sixty chapters, ~2,250 average. Nothing is under the 1,300 floor.** Nine chapters sit above the
2,700 guide (ch 126, 127, 129, 130, 131, 132, 135, 140, 150 — longest **ch 140 at 3,193**) and two sit below the
1,800 guide (**ch 138 at 1,779 and ch 151 at 1,653**). Counts are body prose only, `##` headings removed.
**The Batch 0004 record's claim that every chapter was inside the guide was inaccurate and is corrected here
and in `state/continuity.md`. The guide is a guide; nothing in the volume is padded and nothing is truncated.**

**No bare Settlement fraction went on the page in Volume 3.** Channel was named twice in Batch 0003 (about
ten and the price at ch 149, leaving about five and a half; about three at ch 160 with the thirty-six days of
rationed use accounted for on the page) and **once in Batch 0004, at ch 178: about ten days.** Volume 4
speaks in weeks too.

**The crew is nine men and her. Ten sleepers in twelve bunks.** Never written as ten men.

**Do not let her forgive him.** Non-forgiveness runs from ch 100 through Volume 3 **into the opening of
Volume 4** — that is the year the milestone requires, and ch 178 does not resolve it.

### Where Volume 4 opens — all of it is on the page at ch 178

> **A Warden's office outside the bound now holds her name, her stages and her hours — in order —
> certified by her own county. The dismantling it has ordered can only be executed, and the Foundation
> relicensing she has petitioned can only be countersigned, past a boundary no county paper crosses.**

The rest of it, all read off the block above: **she is at Condensing 9 with a ceiling of nine and nothing
above nine on the document anywhere in the world** · the Foundation petition is **barred and not
withdrawable** on the ground that **this county keeps no schedule of work done past its boundary** ·
the district has answered the Hunter's request with **an empty space the width of a line** · **about
ten days of channel** against **about six weeks of winter still to come in it** · **Ledger is hers to
order and she has not ordered it** · **a date is entered against the eight hundred jin in a counting
room in Lowwater** · the season's road has been shut since day 117 and has not opened.

**The Wall has never been described.** In Volume 3 it existed only as *a writ this county does not
hold*, said once, in Bai Zhu's mouth at ch 174. **Nothing Volume 4 owns was spent in Volume 3.** The
outline is `outline/volume-04.md`; the first batch is planned at `workspace/volume-04-batch-0001/`.

**What changed that could not have changed before.** A woman at the top of the second realm stands at a
cold end at the ninth hour and says it out loud in front of nine men, **and nine counties know exactly
where she stands fourteen days before a Warden's office learns it.** The only paid work in the county
ends and takes a tin of money with it, and she spends the last of that money on a thing nobody will ever
read on paper. A crew discovers that its silence was distance, and then a man of the crew stands in her
one empty room and does not leave, and she does not send him away. She tells nine men out loud that the
hand she signs her hours with is gone, and one of them says *then say so at the frame and I will hold
it*. A registrar enters a nine and then discovers that her licence has nothing left on it. She signs a
column of nine lines with three holes in it, in front of a queue, for eleven minutes. **A patient man who
has never lied to her certifies in writing that she is hunted, and sends it up past every office she has
ever stood in front of, because he is not permitted to do anything else.** She files the last application
this county can take, gets it barred with a number on it, and cannot take it back. And a licence goes up
straight above a bunk, which is the only thing in the volume she is still able to fix.

**Canon changes made in Batch 0004, recorded here and not made silently: 53–66.** All fourteen are set
out in full in `state/continuity.md` under *Established facts, Volume 3 Batch 0004*. In summary: the quarry
economics and the pay-off (53) · what she has been buying and why it is ninety-six coppers (54) · the cold
end is not hers and the crew's silence was distance (55) · the notice and the registrar's line under it (56)
· **Qin, new minor** (56) · the tower and the stair carried by another man's hands (57) · the announcement
and its price (58) · the hand said out loud to nine men (59) · the licence has nothing left on it (60) ·
the reading in order (61) · the certification and the referral (62) · the Foundation petition and the bar
(63) · what went up and what came down (64) · the licence out of the oilcloth and the practice (65) · the
final image and the ten days (66).

**No chapter was replaced, no card beat moved, the single bracket stayed at ch 171, the irreversible step
stayed at ch 171, and every ending stayed where it was.**
