### Volume 3 Batch 0004 — review pass and repair (fix phase)

`logs/volume-03-batch-0004.review.log` reviewed the finished batch, ch 164–178, and found it clean on word
counts, paragraph length, speech, markdown, banned reflexes, the single bracket, the crew count, the ch 171
irreversible step and the ch 178 final image. Four items were carried into this fix pass. **Three are
closed. One is the controller's to close, not an agent's.** No chapter was restarted and no planned plot moved.

| # | Finding | Correction | Plot changed? |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| R1 | **Blocker — `workspace/volume-03-batch-0004/` had no `.done`,** so both selectors take the first eligible directory in sort order and would re-run the finished writer phase and never dispatch `volume-03-close`. | **Left to the controller, deliberately.** `PHASE_SYSTEM.md`: *only the controller may mark a phase done or blocked*, and `scripts/novel_runner.sh` writes `.done` and clears `.attempts .deferred .retry-after .wip-conflict` on the success path the moment this pass returns. **An agent creating the marker would be the exact failure the rule forbids.** Nothing in the manuscript or the state files needed for it. | No. |
| R2 | **A named weekday in three chapters, while the close audit promised *no weekday named anywhere in ch 119–178*.** `Thursday` ×4 at ch 123, ×4 at ch 128, ×2 at ch 131 — all ten forbidden by the four Volume 3 batch cards, and **only ch 131 was on the standing-closed list.** | **Repaired in the chapters, not excused in the prompt.** ch 123's four → *by the end of the week* / *inside a week* / *by the end of the week* / *inside the week*; ch 128's → *on the day the crate went*, and its signing rule re-cast as **the day after the crate goes**, which ch 131 still pays off; ch 131's two → *in the morning of the day the crate went* and *in a bundle on the day the crate went*. **The rule survives without the name.** ch 128's duplicated sentence — *Thursday is the day after the crate goes*, said twice in three lines — is now one statement. | No. |
| R3 | **“Eleven weeks” was a volume-wide tic being locked in rather than fixed** — 17 chapters carried it, four uses each at ch 152, ch 163 and ch 173, and at ch 153 (day 147) and ch 161 (day 178) it stood as elapsed-so-far, which the day table does not support. | **Three chapters traded for relative counts.** ch 153 → **in all the weeks of this cold**, twice, keeping his exact echo of her sentence; ch 161 → **at a counter in this room since the thaw** and **had bought one thing off a county and nothing since**; ch 173's *I have not asked you one question in eleven weeks*, thirty lines from a near-identical *I have asked you nothing in eleven weeks*, → **at any counter in this county**. Ch 173 goes four to three and the doubled construction is gone. **Left on purpose:** ch 163, where the figure is the *subject* of the chapter's accounting and not a tic; ch 152, where the figure is the lane's life; ch 174's *learned eleven weeks earlier*, which is exact against day 133 → 212; ch 178's *Not eleven weeks of winter*, the volume's last negation of the figure. | No. |
| R4 | Minor: **ch 177 carries no quoted dialogue at all** — the only such chapter in the batch. | **Considered and deliberately not changed.** It is one woman alone in a wash house with a licence, then alone with a hammer, and every beat is an action or a decision; dialogue would have meant inventing a second party and weakening the practice the chapter is built on. Recorded so the close audit does not open it as a fault. | No. |

and **four were faults in the state files themselves**, which are corrected here and in
`state/continuity.md`, `state/open-threads.md`, `state/chapter-summaries.md` and `state/book-metadata.md`. No

below supersedes this one wherever the two disagree. **Counts after repair: Batch 0001 36,809 · Batch 0002

The half that was dismantled is live as **about ten days of channel against about six weeks of winter still to come in it** (ch 178 § *How Much Was Left*), with the Foundation petition barred and not withdrawable (ch 175). ### Repairs made in the chapters during this audit

**Nine word- or number-level repairs, in seven chapters.** **No batch was replaced, no card beat moved, the
single bracket stayed at ch 171, the irreversible step stayed at ch 150, and every ending stayed where it
was.** Six of the nine were one figure, put at four where it should have been nine; two are Hou's counting
room being put nine counties away when it is **in Lowwater**, where she is standing; one is the winter's
length at ch 176. **Three of the six were the wrong half of the figure and were reverted at the close
review** — see *Close review repair* below. The audit's own statement of the rule is superseded there.

| # | Ch | Fault | Change |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | 150 | **"about four hundred people in four counties who had heard a woman's body get to six and get to seven"** — the sentence opens *Nine counties* and then puts the announcement in four. **Correct: what is *heard* is nine counties** (V1 ch 46, *in four counties of nine*; ch 55, *going round nine counties*) | **nine counties.** Its echo at ch 161 repaired with it, so the two still echo exactly. **Stands at the close review.** |
| 2 | 153 | **"It had been dramatic in four counties."** The announcement, at four in the morning | **nine counties.** **Stands at the close review.** |
| 3 | 160 | **"And there was a hole in a column four counties wide."** The stage column, two lines under *no sound in nine counties* | **REVERTED at the close review to four counties.** The audit's change to nine was wrong: this is the **written** column, and V1 canon puts documents in four counties |
| 4 | 161 | **"about four hundred people in four counties who had heard her body get to six and get to seven"** — the echo of ch 150 line 143 | **nine counties.** **Stands at the close review.** |
| 5 | 163 | **"A hole in a stage column in four counties"** — the audit changed this to nine on the authority of ch 132 line 217 | **REVERTED at the close review to four counties.** See *Close review repair* below |
| 6 | 163 | **"a date against it nine counties away in a counting room she had never been in"** — Hou Sanliang's counting room is **in Lowwater** (ch 135 line 57, ch 147 line 77, ch 155, ch 173 line 159) | **"in a counting room she had never been in"**, matching ch 173. **Stands at the close review.** |
| 7 | 176 | **"a paper in four counties with three holes in it"** — the audit changed this to nine | **REVERTED at the close review to four counties.** A paper is paper |
| 8 | 176 | **"about eleven weeks of winter left in it"** — four days before ch 178, which says **about six weeks of winter still to come in it**. Both figures land on the same end of the winter (day 184 + 11 weeks = day 215 + 7 weeks = day 219 + 6 weeks), so ch 176 was the wrong one | **about seven weeks.** **Stands at the close review.** |
| 9 | 177 | **"sitting nine counties away in a counting room she had never been in"** — the same, at the last look she takes at the claim before the volume ends | **sitting in a counting room she had never been in.** **Stands at the close review.** |

**Six words left the manuscript and nothing else did.** The four-county figure survives where it belongs —
the claim at ch 132 ×2, 140, 148 ×2; the furnace schedule at ch 148 and 159; the Bureau's bundles at
ch 138; the sponsors' book at ch 128. **At the close review this paragraph and the repair rows above were
corrected: the footprint rule was the wrong way round, and three of the nine repairs were reverted.** Read
*Close review repair* below before using any figure from this audit.

**Verified after repair:** all sixty files have balanced speech; **zero** blockquotes, code fences, tables or
rules; **zero** weekdays; **zero** banned reflexes (*his eyes widened*, *he couldn't believe it*, *suddenly*,
*little did he know*); **zero** authorial scaffolding (*this chapter*, *this volume*, *the next chapter*, *in
this batch*, *the whole series*, *the reader*); four bracketed lines, one each at 135, 140, 148, 171; longest
paragraph 90 words; **Volume 3 file set 135,014 words** — Batch 0001 36,809 · Batch 0002 36,742 · Batch 0003
30,958 · Batch 0004 30,505. All sixty titles unique against 1–178 and none restates weather, hour, date or age.

### Findings recorded and NOT repaired, with the reason

1. **Du Hong's name is used four times in Volume 3** — ch 119 line 77 (*Du Hong paid me in a coat he stole*),
   ch 125 line 87, ch 138 line 89 (Feng's guilt over a false sheet), ch 160 line 19 (the suppression, *she
   worked the same grief with which she had worked Du Hong*). The outline's resolution list says **their
   names are not used again**; Cao Lei and Xu An are indeed never named in Volume 3, and Du Hong is used four
   times, all of them as remembered weight inside a live scene. **The burial line is not reopened**: no
   cause is re-argued, nothing is buried again, and no dead name goes into a document. **Not repaired, and
   the reason is recorded: removing the name damages four good beats to satisfy a line whose operative
   purpose — the burial line stays shut — is already satisfied.** Recorded here so it is not rediscovered as
   an unknown fault.
2. **"Eleven weeks" stands in fourteen chapters, twenty-eight uses, and carries at least five different
   senses** — the Hunter's span in this county (ch 150, 162, 163, 173, 174), weeks of the lane (152), weeks
   of savings (152), weeks of winter remaining (163), and a projection (151). **The state record was wrong
   on this.** The Batch 0004 fix pass traded it out of ch 153 and ch 161 (confirmed: **neither chapter carries
   it now**), reduced ch 173 from four uses to three, and left it deliberately at ch 150, 162, 163 and 174.
   The audit's sweep found it in **fourteen** chapters, not four. **The one instance that contradicted the
   final image — ch 176 — is repaired above.** The rest is **left standing by explicit instruction** and
   **carried out of the volume as a retired number**: *no new use of "eleven weeks" is permitted in Volume 4,
   and no elapsed figure may be built on it.* Where a Volume 4 chapter needs the Hunter's span, it is
   re-derived from **day 2 (his arrival, ch 119) to the day in question**.
3. **"About four seconds" is the volume's standard pause-length and appears thirty-six times across
   twenty-six chapters**, with a maximum of three in any one chapter (135, 148, 162) and never twice in a row.
   The Batch 0004 record claimed *one use at ch 171 plus the one-word beat*; that is **inaccurate** and is
   corrected below. **This is not a rule violation**: the four seconds and the release rule were established
   at ch 53–54 as his unit of speech, and none of these thirty-six is a re-explanation of that rule — they are
   ordinary elapsed time, varied by the situation, and the *release rule itself* is never re-explained.
   **Recorded, not repaired.** It is a rhythm, not a rule, and cutting thirty-six uses across a closed volume
   would be a batch-level intervention this phase is not authorised to make.
4. **Ch 177 carries no dialogue at all** — the only such chapter in the volume. Already adjudicated at the
   Batch 0004 review (item R4) and deliberately unchanged: one woman alone in a wash house with a licence,
   then alone with a hammer. **Confirmed here and not reopened.**
5. **Ch 166 ends on "She signed for her own shift and went to work."** Read against the paragraph above it —
   the cold end is the coldest place in the building, she is not posted there and it is not the crew's to
   post — this is the enactment of a decision, not a neutral action. **Passes.**

  1,779 and **ch 151 is 1,653**, both below the 1,800 guide; nine chapters are above 2,700. Nothing is under
  the 1,300 floor. Corrected in `state/continuity.md`, `state/book-metadata.md` and this file.
- **"The *eleven weeks* figure now stands at ch 150, 162, 163 and 174 only"** — **wrong.** It stands in
  **fourteen chapters**. Corrected above and in `state/continuity.md`.
- **"The four seconds: one use at ch 171"** — **wrong.** Thirty-six uses across twenty-six chapters.
  Corrected in `state/continuity.md`.
- **Volume 3 file set** — 135,020 → **135,014** after the audit's repairs. Restated in
  `state/book-metadata.md`, `state/continuity.md`, `state/open-threads.md` and
  `workspace/volume-03-close/SUMMARY.md`.
- **"The name in the wrong order: five uses across four chapters (120 ×2, 129, 158, 162)"** — **wrong, and
  wrong in the close audit's own sweep.** It is **six uses across five chapters**: ch 120:15, ch 129:27,
  ch 129:29, **ch 149:5**, ch 158:15, ch 162:65. The second ch 120 hit is stones put back on a mat in the
  wrong order, not the name. Corrected in `state/continuity.md` and above.
- **"The footprint of the stage column is nine counties"** — **wrong, and reversed at the close review.**
  Nine is the heard footprint, four is the written one. See *Close review repair* below.

fixes her at **eighteen in Volume 3** and ch 131 is in Volume 3. **Repaired at the close review to

### The construction-budget sweep

(the Batch 0004 record claimed one at ch 164; the actual line there is *he was not thanked*, and ch 164 line 57
reads *four of them done by men who did not know what she was* — **no instance of the construction exists**).
**Nobody said anything: nine uses across eight chapters** — 125, 131 ×2, 134, 154, 159, 162, 172, 174 — four of
them inside Batch 0003 and five inside Batch 0004, never twice in one chapter except ch 131. That is one use
per seven and a half chapters and none is load-bearing twice; **recorded as at the top of the acceptable

happy life*: zero.** **Weekdays: zero.** **Authorial scaffolding: zero.** **`his eyes widened`,

enemy.**

### Close review repair — this block supersedes the footprint rule above

The volume-close review re-audited this phase and **reversed the central rule the close audit wrote.** The
audit held that *nine counties* was the footprint of the stage column and the relicensed licence and that
*four counties* was the claim's and the furnace schedule's. **That is the wrong way round, and it breaks four
closed Volume 1 chapters.**

**The canon, from Volume 1, in the characters' own words:**

- **ch 46** — *the same advancement, **in four counties of nine**. Hanhui wrote that it was done for
  discipline. Shouyi wrote that no name had been given at all.*
- **ch 55** — *The announcement had been going round **nine counties** for three days, and Du Hong had read
  **her four counties' versions**.*
- **ch 53** — *somewhere in **four counties** four copyists were writing down four sentences about it.*
- **ch 55** — *There were **four copies of it in four counties**, because the announcement had gone out to four
  counties and each had written it down in its own book.*
- **ch 58** — *The design was on a document **in four counties**.*

**So: NINE counties is what is heard. FOUR counties is what is written.** Volume 2 says the same in one
sentence (ch 115): *An announcement is audible inside the bound.* Volume 3's own unedited text says it twice —
**ch 148:51** and **ch 159:61** both put the stage column and the annex on a furnace schedule **in four
counties**, in the same sentence as *nine counties* knowing about it.

**What the audit's authority actually was, and is not.** The audit cited ch 132:217 — *readable in order by
anybody in nine counties with a stamp and a reason*. That sentence is about **obtainability**: with a stamp
and a reason, a person in any county may get a certified copy and read it. **It is not a statement about
which books the column is entered in**, and it cannot license putting the column itself in nine books.

**Repaired here — three one-word reverts, ch 160, ch 163, ch 176.** All three are now back at **four
counties**: *a hole in a column four counties wide* (ch 160), *A hole in a stage column in four counties*
(ch 163), *a paper in four counties with three holes in it* (ch 176). **The three heard-footprint repairs
stand** — ch 150, ch 153 and ch 161 are about what people *heard*, which is nine. **Ch 178's *nine counties
that had it in order* stands** and is the reading, not the books; ch 171 (*It went out of the bound in every
voice in nine counties*) is the volume's idiom for it.

**This makes Volume 3 harsher, not softer, and that is correct.** One advancement entered in four counties is
entered four times and billed four times. That is what happened to her father at ch 55 and it is what the
three holes in her own column now do to her, in four county books, permanently.

**Rule as it now stands, for Volume 4:** nine for hearing, reading out, going round, or a certified copy
obtainable with grounds; four for anything written into a book. The rule is in `state/open-threads.md`.

**Also at this review:** ch 131's **"She was twenty."** repaired to **"She was eighteen."** against
`bible/characters.md` (*seventeen at the start of Volume 1; eighteen in Volume 3*). One word. See
*Closed at the close review* in `state/continuity.md`.

**And one operational finding, not repaired because it is not this phase's to touch:**
`workspace/volume-03-close/` had **no `.done` marker**, where `volume-03-batch-0003` and `-0004` both had
one. `.done` is written by the runner on the success path — every `novel: complete <phase>` commit in this
repository contains that one empty file and nothing else — and is a controller artifact.
**Recorded, not touched** — if a close phase re-dispatches, that is the runner's business, not a writing
phase's.

**Same finding, restated at the close-record review, and still not touched:**
`workspace/volume-03-close-record/` also carries no `.done`, and
`logs/volume-03-close-record.review.log` confirms the dispatcher's skip rule at
`.github/workflows/novels.yml` will treat that directory as pending, because its first prompt line is
`# Volume 3 close record` and matches neither the `outline` nor the `phase` token the skip rule looks for.
**No marker was created here.** The runner writes it when this review/fix pass returns successfully, and
a writing phase must not pre-empt it.

### The Volume 4 hand-off

The audit does not plan Volume 4. It records what is **true on the page at ch 178** and Volume 4 opens on it:

> **A Warden's office outside the bound now holds her name, her stages and her hours — in order — certified
> by her own county. The dismantling it has ordered can only be executed, and the Foundation relicensing she
> has petitioned can only be countersigned, past a boundary no county paper crosses.**

And alongside it, all of it on the page:

- **She is at Condensing 9, the top of the second realm, with a ceiling of nine and nothing above it on the
  document.** Sun He said so out loud in a room (ch 173) and a registrar put it in a county's book. The next
  stage is Foundation the first stage, and the paper that would carry her there is **petitioned, numbered,
  received, routed, barred and not withdrawable** (ch 175).
- **The district has already answered once**: received, numbered, referred upward, no instrument held in the
  district either, and an empty space the width of a line on a public shelf by a door (ch 176).
- **About ten days of channel**, said once, in Volume 3, in weeks, in the final image. **Ledger is hers to
  order and his to hold only on her word, and she has never ordered it.** **She does not forgive him and she
  does not tell him to stop.**
- **A date is entered against the eight hundred jin in a counting room she has never been in.** Hou Sanliang
  is unpaid and patient and has never been met.
- **Nothing Volume 4 owns was spent in Volume 3**: no description of the Wall, no crossing, no geography past
  a marker post, no Warden but his field agent's signature and the name of his office, which **Yuan still
  cannot read and has now said four times he cannot and is not going to say it a fifth time to be different
  about it** (ch 178).

**What changed that could not have changed before.** A woman refused a true mercy in a county office in
front of two witnesses and signed the refusal herself, and the refusal is now on her member's sheet, on
the furnace schedule, and in a crew's hands, and in two books under a man's name. A door she verified was
real is shut behind her and nothing in the volume opens it again. A man with a room over a tanner's door
has his name in a Warden's book and has never been called anything else in nine counties until a clerk
wrote it. She is at eight with a hole in a column and an office above this county reads the hole as
concealment and is right. A man at a board behind a well has six frames out of eight because of a word
she put on the front of a schedule, and four of her crew are quietly paying it back. And the thing that
can finish her is not in this county, and a request for it is sitting on a shelf with a number on it.

**Canon change 41 — the four terms of the handover (ch 150), all true.** Voice certified as a misread
natural echo on a named day before the end of the winter · designation drawn down over one winter,
without injury and without a stage taken · licence, ceiling, conditions, defect and seventh condition all
standing · **the eight hundred jin answered out of the Warden's own purse, entered as a claim satisfied
by a third party, which pays Hou Sanliang in full on the day.** Read once, used, never re-listed.

**Canon change 42 — the two questions and true answers (ch 150).** *Who pays:* the Warden's office, its
own purse; **not this county, not the three sponsors**; the money goes to the creditor. *What happens to
the thing in her head:* **it goes out**, on a date inside the winter; **he cannot tell her what becomes of
it and says so plainly** and adds that it does not hurt, which is a term and not a comfort.

**Canon change 43 — the fourth line of the second sheet (ch 150).** *Announcements already carried to the
bound are not comprised in this instrument and are not affected by it.* Plain, in the ordinary place, not
hidden, written by a man who did not put it in and would not have taken it out. **This is why she
refuses.** The mercy is real, painless, and pays a creditor she has never met, and it leaves four
announcements standing with nobody left alive to explain them.

**Canon change 44 — the naming (ch 150).** The handover paper is headed and signed by the Warden's office
and carries **no agent's name.** A clerk bringing a Warden's book out of a press under the counter has a
line that wants the agent who laid the paper, asks for it, and **Bai Zhu is named — once, by the agent,
to a form.** Sun He writes the same two syllables into the county book. **He is named in no other chapter
of the book and on no document he signs.**

**Canon change 45 — the monthly attendance (ch 151).** Under a refusal entered in a Warden's book, a
member who has declined an instrument laid by an officer of that office **attends that office once a
month**, in person, signed, on a fixed day. It does not touch the licence; it is on her member's sheet,
which goes up with the schedule. **A missed attendance is entered separately from her hours and cannot be
paid for in hours.**

**Canon change 46 — the monthly reading (ch 153, 161).** A quarterly return is signed; a monthly
attendance is **read** and then signed. Sun He reads the stage column aloud and states the ruling **to the
room and not to her**, the second time, with the Hunter present, and enters it. **He is not defending her;
he is putting the ruling on the record beside a man who said the opposite thing at a counter in June, and
both sentences are now in the same county's book.**

**Canon change 47 — the signing hand (ch 154).** The **heel of her left hand** at the base of the thumb,
off a wet sledge handle while lifting a load off a man on ice. She can hold; she cannot turn. About a
third of every load's pay is gone and does not come back. **A county hour is in person and in her own
hand, so a ruined hand is a filing problem**; she writes the column with the **flat of the second finger**,
finished by a drag out to the right — slower, legible, hers — eleven minutes longer on every county hour,
and nobody in nine counties will ever know.

**Canon change 48 — the weight ticket (ch 158).** The lane had **carriers' run lists entered by the
weigh-house**; the quarry had none, because it pays by a weight written at the rope. **A quarry hour was
uncertifiable, so the annex did not fill, and a gap in that column is work done and not shown — which is
not an absence.** **Kan** writes the hour in at the left and the load in at the right on one line, at the
cost of being the man holding the pen. Forty-one cards, irregular, stamped — the word on the front of the
Qinghe furnace schedule for the fourth time in a year.

**Canon change 49 — Da Shan (ch 162).** He was asked what a furnace schedule was and **read the crew's own
sheet off his own wall, the way a man reads a bill.** The mechanism is a word she put on the front of a
schedule herself. She answers it by putting four of her crew on his lane on their own time without asking
him; **Gao says her name out loud when he asks the second time**, as she told him he would; Da Shan puts
the frame back himself and **never asks her a question.**

**Canon change 50 — the suppressed stage (ch 160).** Condensing 8, at her own order, in an empty hut at a
quarry, in the ordinary voice, to a stove. **No bracketed line and nothing in nine counties.** What the
filing records is an hour worked. **A suppressed stage leaves exactly the same absence as any other hole:
the same width.** Sun He's precedent holds it as absence; a reader above this county reads it as
concealment and is right, and nothing stops it.

**Canon change 51 — Ledger's standing (ch 160).** **From ch 150 Ledger is hers to order and his to hold
only on her word.** Stated once on the page: *this time it is not me deciding that I will not. It is me
not being allowed to.* The first thing in the year that is different, and she did it on purpose.

**Canon change 52 — the request on the shelf (ch 163).** A county office keeps a public shelf of
everything that has gone up and anybody may read it. On it: **a numbered, dated request for an instrument
not held in this county, for use in this county, after the reading of the winter return.** Nothing sent,
nothing filed, the answer space empty. **A field agent cannot unmake a System inside a bound without a
Wall writ**, which is why he attended two readings, bought one thing off a county and never asked a
question. **The referral itself is not filed and not spoken: that is ch 169–175.**

**Recorded, not repaired — two faults in Batch 0002, both standing and neither reopened.** The closing line
of **ch 131 reads “She was twenty.”** The smallest repair would be to cut the number or move it into the
surrounding clause; **`bible/characters.md` fixes her at eighteen in Volume 3 and Batch 0003 states her age
nowhere on the page** (two ages were caught in Batch 0003 draft — at ch 156 and ch 163 — and cut).
**Ch 131 also named a weekday** (*on a Thursday in the morning*); Batch 0003 names **zero** weekdays and
two were caught in draft and cut. **Neither chapter was opened when this batch was written. Both were
opened at the Batch 0004 fix pass and the weekday is gone** — see *Volume 3 Batch 0004 — review pass and
repair (fix phase)*, item **R2**. *She was twenty* at ch 131 stood untouched through this batch and **was
repaired at the close review** — see *Close review repair*.

| **No stage above five. Zero bracketed layer lines. No Ledger. No Merit. No tribulation** | **done** — Condensing 5 for all fourteen chapters; **zero brackets in the batch**, so ch 135's announcement is the layer's first line in twenty-one chapters; nothing on the page touches a price |

**What changed that could not have changed before.** She is relicensed to nine and the claim is on
**two** stamps. A man now holds her hours, her ceiling and her annex address in order for the cost of
one coin. Three men who have owed her nothing have bought her a ceiling by taking a price in silence.
The hunt has left the paper and entered the lane. And the one weapon of her life has been put on a
table and not fired, which is irreversible in the other direction.

**Canon change 31 — the relicensed licence, fixed on the page at ch 132.** Clause Four. Ceiling at
Condensing nine in her own hand. Defect re-entered in her own hand with a new line under it: *the
defect restores nothing to the aperture. It is entered here again because this is a new document and a
document carries what it carries.* Seven conditions: the six carried forward, the second amended so the
certified annex is furnace hours by certification and not another schedule, a new sixth (annex hours
return with the quarter, on the same crate in the same order), and **the eight hundred jin as the
seventh and the last thing on the paper**. **Read once. Referenced, never re-listed.**

**Canon change 32 — the annex, fixed on the page at ch 124 and paid at ch 130.** Hours worked outside
the crew, written onto the Qinghe furnace schedule by the Hall's copyist, certified by the Hall's clerk
and stamped by a maintaining member, and returned at the quarter, **are furnace-schedule hours by
certification and not by place**. Legal since the clause was written; nobody in nine counties has paid
for it before. It is what lets the second condition be amended without being broken, and it is why the
asked figure has to be level at the close of the season — **fifty-eight hours, written in her own hand,
signed without looking up.**

**Canon change 33 — the Hunter's first purchase and his room (ch 126, 127).** A certified copy of the
Qinghe furnace schedule at the county office, coin on open wood, receipt kept in his own hand and
folded square against the counter edge. A room over the tanner's under **Chou Zhyan**, a clerk's
misspelling written by a woman who cannot read from the lodger's own mouth. Five times on Feng's fourth
stair and never once a question. **One purchase in five and a half weeks. Unnamed throughout.**

**Recorded, not repaired — the Settlement column.** `outline/volume-03.md` gives a running fraction
(0.75 → 0.82 → 0.70) that rises with use, while ch 118's published line reads *a little under
three-quarters* of **remaining channel**, which falls with use. The two are in tension. **The page
resolves it by speaking only in weeks from ch 121 onward, and ch 132 states about three weeks once.**
No bare fraction is put on the page again in Volume 3. Recorded here rather than silently reconciled,
because the alternative was editing a closed volume's arithmetic for a planning table.

`outline/volume-03.md`, before any chapter. Sixteen chapters, 133–148, **36,742 words, average ~2,296.**

**What changed that could not have changed before.** A listener is copying her hours upward truly for
the rest of his life and he took the wage that pays him for it. A man who could have stood on a fourth
stair once more will never have to. A second body in this county has her hand on it and the eight
hundred jin has not moved. The word *irregular* is on the front of a schedule nine men read. The season's
road is shut. And the stair costs three counts, which it did not cost in the spring.

**Canon change 34 — the irregular annex (ch 133).** The Hall's copyist had been certifying the paid-work
annex by converting the hirer's **loads** at the Hall's two-armed rate. Her own slate frame, written out
by her in her own left hand every day, is certified beside it and the Hall clerk writes ***irregular*** on
the front of the Qinghe furnace schedule and stamps it. The crew reads its own schedule. **Cost: an hour
of the furnace day and four miles, every day, forever.**

**Canon change 35 — the scope line's third half, read at last (ch 134).** A member licensed to practise
past registration **may be taught past her stage, under countersignature, at that county.** She carried
the licence four months without noticing. No master in nine counties will put a hand to it, and she
builds the stage herself out of load — which is the thing ch 140's tax then takes.

**Canon change 36 — the escort-road pace (ch 140).** Five weeks of a road with a train on it builds a
**sequence**: where a wheel begins to lean, a quarter of a mile out. Spent as Merit at ch 140, told
first, **out of her foundation, unrelearnable**, and its absence is on the page at ch 144 as a wheel she
cannot see.

**Canon change 37 — the Listener's appointment, four lines (ch 141, taken 143).** Reports the Lantern
Register's paper upward truly and in full, at the quarter, in the form in which it is carried, **whether
or not it is entered, and whether or not the keeper's hand is in it**; an omission is entered against the
office and not the keeper; **for life**; **set aside on request from without the bound, without the
Listener's consent, on the same terms as a designation.** In exchange: a district wage quarterly, oil, a
hundredweight of coal to the door, and a chair in the county office on reading days with the county's
fire. **The offer comes from the Hunter, not from the Bureau, and it is not in the county's book.**
**The second Lung is Volume 11 and is unspent.**

**Canon change 38 — the guard's hire (ch 144).** Twenty coppers a day and a quarter of the train's
share, five weeks to the close of the season's road, eight men on a train and a ninth on the bridge,
**the road shuts with the weather and not with the contractor.** Inside Clause Four's scope, and
therefore annexed, certified and stamped like everything else. Paid at the two-armed rate, worked at a
third of it.

**Canon change 39 — the second hand (ch 146).** Escort loads carry no hour. A **carriers' run list**,
entered by the weigh-house rather than by whoever holds the slate, is the second hand the county accepts.
**The girl of twenty-four has been copying about two hundred of them since the thaw and nobody has ever
wanted one.** Paid on the day of hand-over and not before; not thanked.

**Canon change 40 — the suspension line (ch 139).** One quarter off the compilation and entry of the
quarterly upward bundle; the bundle carried by a clerk of the county office; **the volumes, the copying
floor, the column, the stipend and the house all stay**; and *the district does not terminate an
examination.* **He is not dismissed, and dismissal would end an observation.**

**Recorded, not repaired — two faults in a closed batch, found while drafting this one.** Neither chapter
was opened.

| Ch | Fault | Note |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 131 | The closing line reads **“She was twenty.”** | `bible/characters.md` fixes her at **seventeen at the start of Volume 1, eighteen in Volume 3**, and the manuscript's own elapsed time across Volumes 1–3 is about fourteen months, which does not reach twenty. The smallest repair would be to cut the number or move it into the surrounding clause. **Not edited** — Batch 0001 is closed and the batch prompt forbids reopening 119–132. **Batch 0002 therefore states her age nowhere on the page**, and where a character has to have one (the bounty man's old description at ch 140) it reads *a woman of about eighteen*, which agrees with the bible. **CLOSED at the close review: repaired to “She was eighteen.”** |

Volume 3 Batch 0002's own hard rule is **no weekday anywhere**, and two were caught in draft and cut; this one predates it. **Not edited then** — Batch 0001 was closed and the batch prompt forbade reopening 119–132. **REPAIRED at the Batch 0004 fix pass:** read **“in the morning of the day the crate went”**, with ch 128's signing rule re-cast as *the day after the crate goes* and ch 128's bundle line as *on the day the crate went*, so the rule ch 131 pays off survives without the name. **ch 119–178 now names no weekday at all.** |

**After repair:** 28,234 words over fifteen chapters, average ~1,882. Every speech closed, one bracket
in the batch, no paragraph over 90 words, no title changed. **No canon change resulted from the
repairs** — every one of them moves a figure back onto an existing canonical fact rather than
creating a new one.

### Checked and deliberately not repaired

- **Ch 110's *four hundred years old*** (the struck word's precedent) is kept. Ch 96 fixes the last
  entry before hers at four hundred years back, so it is a different figure from the six-hundred-year
  span of the run and is correct where it stands.
- **Ch 115's *twelfth day after the burial*** against ch 104's tenth-day start and fourth morning
  out. Defensible either way — reached on the twelfth, found out on the thirteenth — and churning
  either line would be a coin-flip on good prose.
- **The *two hundred and forty hands* in ch 114**, which echoes the tower figure from ch 108, is not
  a stated contradiction: nobody has ever counted the hands through the settling floor.
- **Yuan's past-tense clauses about completed events** are the ch 57 convention, not a Volume 2 slip.
- **The calendar.** The manuscript's own anchors reconcile (ch 54's *about four months* → ch 80's
  *six and a half weeks* → ch 99's *five days* → ch 101's *eleven weeks* → ch 118's *six weeks*; ch
  115's *eleven weeks* against ch 116's *six weeks ago*; ch 114's *six weeks to the return* against
  ch 118's *five weeks*). The **plan** called the volume four months and the manuscript has run to
  about five and a half, which is why ch 116 says *From the sixth of December to the last day of the
  thaw* and ch 118 closes in the first week of summer. The page is internally consistent; the
  planning figure was the thing that moved. Recorded, not repaired. **One exception, repaired since:
  the *second week of summer* for the first return in ch 116 and ch 118 was never supported by any
  calendar, and is now *the fifth week of summer* — see Canon change 30.**

title, no bracket, no ending, and the ch 150 refusal is neither softened nor re-decided anywhere.**

was moved onto the table.** The refusal is **day 136**; the first attendance is **day 147**; the gap is
**eleven days** and every chapter in the batch now reads it that way.

the Hunter's run was used six times across the batch, is not supported by the Volume 3 day table, and was
already the standing figure for a spent interval elsewhere. That review accepted it as internally

## Volume 3 — the two open structural questions

> **Both are now decided, in `outline/volume-03.md` (*The relicensing*).** Route through the
> sponsors; clearance (ch 130) → three sponsors' seal (131) → Sun He's countersignature (132);
> ceiling at Condensing 9 in her own hand; seven conditions with the claim re-entered last.
> The material below is the gathered reasoning that produced those decisions and is kept for the
> record. Read the outline for the plan, not this section.

Material for the planning phase. **Neither is decided here.** These are the gathered facts and what a
decision would cost, nothing more.

### One — How does she get past the cap?

The licence caps her at **Condensing stage 5** and bars any Foundation petition without relicensing.
`outline/series.md` §4 puts Foundation in **Volumes 3–4**, and §9 puts her at Condensing 9 somewhere
inside them. So Volume 3 has to answer *how the ceiling lifts* or deliberately decline and say so.

**What the manuscript now holds.**

- **The mechanism she named herself, ch 116:** *the Foundation: barred without relicensing, and
  relicensing is a new application, and a new application needs a schedule and a schedule needs an
  hours column and her hours are the furnace's hours, and the furnace does not stop.* Relicensing is
  not a variation on the licence. It is **a second application down the same three legs**: the
  sponsors' seal (ch 62), quota clearance (ch 86), the county countersignature (ch 115).
- **What relicensing would cost in paper.** Ch 115 reads the whole new application aloud: a filing
  with **hours continuous from the day the tithe book opened**, a **defect declared in the
  applicant's own hand**, a **ceiling entered at the fifth stage of Condensing**, and **no petition
  for the Foundation without relicensing**. A fresh filing under a fresh ceiling would have to carry
  a continuous hours record **from the same origin date** — that is, from day 1 of the book opened
  at ch 64. Every hour since is on the Qinghe furnace schedule and is being returned to the county
  at the quarter (ch 118's first return, five weeks out).
- **The two conditions that make relicensing hard, in her own words and the county's.** Condition
  one: **at every county hour, in person, in her own hand, no surety again without relicensing** —
  and ch 106 establishes what that costs: she cannot be ill, cannot be carried, cannot be late, and a
  missed hour with nobody standing is a gap that breaks a filing. Condition two: **hours on the
  Qinghe furnace schedule only, no other schedule counts.** Condition three: nothing above Condensing
  five is licensed — so a relicensing above five needs a **new ceiling in her own hand**, which is
  the same act at ch 88 that killed the sponsors' writ.
- **The writ.** Ch 48: Foundation within two years, outside the ordinary schedule, on the three
  sponsors' signatures. Ch 88: Foundation on that writ reaches only through a licence; without one it
  is unregistered practice under a schedule it does not appear on, which breaches the sponsorship
  that issued it. Ch 113: Lu Kang's answer to being told the writ is dead — *it has not been revoked
  and it has no road left to anywhere.* **The writ is not revoked and it is not usable.** All three
  sponsors know and say nothing, and that silence is a standing position they will hold.
- **The signature problem nobody has said aloud yet.** Leg one of the licence is the sponsors' seal.
  The sponsors' interest in the writ was a Foundation that bypassed the schedule. A relicensing that
  reads **higher** than five is *worse* for them than the one they sealed. **Their seal is the hardest
  signature in the county and it would have to be given for the thing they lost.**
- **Other signatures the county office implies exist.** Registrar **Sun He** holds the county book,
  nineteen years at that table, and has told her what changes a refused paper (labour on the sheet).
  The **Hall clerk at Qinghe** certifies the schedule, and the Hall's copyist has had her name in the
  wrong order since ch 61 and still has no corrected sheet. **Shen Xiu** certifies clearance in the
  quota book and holds the winter book; he has never looked at her and never will.
- **The claim rides on the licence.** Condition six is now the eight hundred jin standing claim,
  travelling with the document and not affected by it. **Any relicensing is a new document, and Sun
  He's ch 115 ruling is the precedent: a claim in a county book cannot be unlisted by a man stamping
  a different document, and he is *required* to enter it.** So a relicensed licence is a licence the
  claim is re-entered on, unless somebody with different authority answers it. **That is the
  mechanism by which relicensing could reopen the bounty, and it is the chapter-level stake.**
- **What relicensing cannot do.** It does not restore the aperture (condition five), does not remove
  the defect line, does not create a surety, does not shorten the road, and — per ch 113 — does not
  revive the Foundation route on a sponsor's signature. A relicensed licence buys a **ceiling**, not a
  body. And the licence is a licence to practise, so every hour of the relicensing is hours taken out
  of the furnace schedule that is the licence's own collateral.

**What a decision would cost, and who would have to sign.** Three of the four routes cost a person.

1. **Through the sponsors.** Cheapest in paper, dearest in position. Needs Lu Kang's seal on a
   ceiling above the one his writ died under, with Deng Chao and Pei An on the same sheet. Cost: she
   goes back to the three men who let her sign at ch 88 and asks them for the thing they refused
   themselves in writing.
2. **Through the county, against the sponsors.** Needs a ceiling above five on a schedule the
   sponsors do not own. The only other schedule in reach is paid work outside the crew — which is
   exactly what the licence's own scope line bought her and exactly what ch 116 shows would put her
   in a lane at the third hour with a paper in her coat and a standing attached to it. Cost: condition
   two, which is currently the thing making her safe.
3. **Through the claim.** Answer the eight hundred jin and the standing that refuses a licence goes
   with it. Cost: a body, a place and a name. Hou Sanliang is unpaid and patient and has never been
   in a room with her, and the eight proofs left are each further away and dearer.
4. **Through Yuan.** Ledger becomes a standing stage in Volume 3 and she can see further than she can
   reach. Cost: he begins deciding for people who did not ask, and she knows that is what Ledger is
   before she spends it.

**Open, and for the planning phase to close:** which route, who signs, and whether the relicensing is
a scene she asks for or a scene somebody else puts in front of her. **Do not decide it here.**

### Two — What is Ledger, on a standing stage?

`outline/series.md` §4 assigns **Ledger to Volumes 3–5**: *read others through her; issue
announcements.* Ch 80 was the one recorded exception and it is now closed. Volume 3 has to decide
what the stage looks like when it stops being an exception and starts being how he operates.

**What the manuscript now holds.**

- **What he lost to get it, ch 80.** Reaching past her skin **takes something back** — it has a name
  and he has not used it, and the name is not the point. When it went up hard last time it cost
  **four and a half weeks of ordinary use**. He has spent it **twice and never told her**. It comes
  out of **her foundation and does not return**.
- **What it bought, ch 80.** Less than a day. Hou Sanliang's man read end to end in one sitting: a
  name he answers to, a broker's counting room, instructions to buy **records and not wordings**,
  coin for desks and coin for writing and **nothing for force** — and one instruction standing
  outside the rest because it carried no coin at all: **a body is not to be asked for, a body is to be
  waited on.**
- **What he lost on giving it back.** *I put it down now and I do not take it again.* Same day,
  permanently, and **on her order**. He also lost, at ch 54, the permanent ability to read past her
  skin unasked. **Between those two, he cannot read anything at all without spending four and a half
  weeks out of her.**
- **What the clock means for it.** At ch 118 the channel is **a little under three-quarters, about six
  weeks.** A single retake costs about **three quarters of what is left.** He cannot do it twice. And
  she has just made it a standing term: *No merit from a thing that happens to me. No reading a man
  through my eyes. No Ledger, and there is no version of the rest of the winter where there is
  Ledger* (ch 118). **Any move to Ledger in Volume 3 is therefore her breaking her own term, in
  writing, made four chapters ago.** That is the beat.
- **What ch 118 forbids her:** *No merit from a thing that happens to me.* Reading a man is not
  merit — it is channel — so a standing Ledger does not violate that clause. **It violates the
  clause above it**, which is about her body and its rate.
- **What a standing stage would make possible, and what it would break.** §4 gives him two powers:
  **read others through her**, and **issue announcements**. Issuing announcements means he writes a
  sentence that goes out bound-wide without her speaking it — which is the ch 54 failure at scale,
  the four seconds she derived the release rule from, and the thing she has never forgiven and is not
  going to. Reading others through her means **she watches through his eyes**, which is a stranger's
  body, and it is the mechanism of the ch 80 intimacy: he looked at the face and she had four seconds
  and used three on it.
- **Who it costs.** At ch 80 it was her knees going watery on a step and forty people watching her
  come down. **Ledger as a standing stage means the reach happens in company, not in a stolen hour.**
  Every room she is in becomes a room he is reading, in front of the people in it.
- **What the Hunter changes about it.** `outline/series.md` §6 item 3: a field agent sent to
  dismantle an **unauthorised System**. The claim against her is that the voice is not sanctioned.
  **The only way to be visibly authorised is to be read by somebody with authority** — and the only
  instrument in the book that reads through a body is him.

**What a decision would cost, and who would have to sign.** It has to be **her**, on her word, in
public, with the four and a half weeks named before she says the word, and it has to buy something
the price is proportionate to. The candidates on the page are: the identity of the man at the first
desk who gives his name to nobody (ch 118); whether the claim can be answered at all; and the office
Yuan cannot read.

**Open, and for the planning phase to close:** when she breaks her own term, what she gets for it,
and what it does to the two of them at the point in the volume where the relationship milestone sits
(`outline/series.md` §7 item 4 — *she chooses to be hunted rather than handed over*). **Do not decide
it here.**

**Canon change 21 — the six licence conditions.** Fixed on the page at ch 115, carried on the licence for the rest of the book. Listed in `state/continuity.md` and `state/open-threads.md`. **Use them; never re-list or re-explain them.**

**Canon change 22 — ch 114 announcement wording.** *Assembly Hall of Qinghe. Ning Yan of the Qinghe furnace crew, servant-born, entered on the furnace schedule of this Hall. Nothing further is let go. Condensing, stage five, at Qinghe is held.* Four-clause order, not ch 53 / 78 / 92. **The third clause carries the ceiling.**

**Canon change 23 — what a surety is for.** Stated once, at ch 106, by a county clerk: not a name on a sheet, but the right to be sick, carried, or late. The dead surety's death took the right to be absent, and a county hour missed with nobody standing is a gap that breaks a filing. She has it written onto the filing at the same hour (no surety again without relicensing) and it becomes condition one of the licence.

**Canon change 24 — the sealed slip identifies the term by position, not characters.** This is how a keeper can act on a request from outside the bound without reading what he is acting on, and it is why the word survives in **two heads** while the paper is emptied. **Feng keeps the shape and not the word.** The volumes on the high shelf are not copies and are **not struck** — the door closes on a shelf, not a silence. The slip wears an ordinary Lowwater county hand and **not** the unrecognised Bureau hand from ch 36.

**Canon change 25 — the claim is on the licence.** Condition six enters the eight hundred jin standing claim on the Licence to Practise Past Registration, where it travels with it and is not affected by it. **This is the volume's last reversal: she is made legal and the bounty is now attached to the document she carries, in a county hand, going up the quarter with everything else.**

**Canon change 26 — Lu Kang's silent stamp (ch 113).** He certifies the furnace schedule himself, reads the whole of it, does not look at the nine households, says nothing. She does not thank him. All three sponsors remain silent on the dead Foundation writ.

**Canon change 27 — Feng's one line at the back of a fair volume (ch 117).** Not entered in the column, which stays at five; entered upward with the quarter and entered here. No audit. No Lung. The Lung is taken in Volume 3.

**Canon change 28 — the Hunter's arrival is a sheet of paper, not a scene (ch 118).** Feng's fair hand comes down with the crate. A second cough in the stairwell, a man at the first desk twice a week, buys nothing, gives his name to nobody, tells carriers he is not from the Bureau. **Unnamed. Bai Zhu is not named in Volume 2.**

**Canon change 29 — Ning Yan's four conditions and Yuan's three (ch 118).** Hers: hours only, no Merit off her body, nothing decided about her body including its rate, no stage above five, and he tells her on the day it is gone. His three, unchanged since the ninth step: he decides nothing about her, he tells her the cost before he spends anything, and if the designation rots he does not hide it and does not get to ask her to hold on. **These are standing terms and may be referred to, never re-listed.**

## Volume 2 — the volume's own accounting, as it now stands on the page

**What she wanted for four months, delivered at ch 115.** The Licence to Practise Past Registration, Clause Four, countersigned by Registrar Sun He at the Bureau of Records' county office at Lowwater, with the season's furnace schedule attached as collateral labour and **Feng Lushi witnessing**. Capped at **Condensing stage 5**. Defect in her own hand: *aperture cracked through; stabilised at the crack; permanent.* Six conditions on the back, the sixth of which is the claim.

**What it cost.** Four months of furnace-crew labour tithe in Shen Xiu's book, in hours by an enemy's clerk. A living surety, who is dead. Her own hours at every county hour with no one standing in the room behind her. **The fourth stage of her life, which is on no paper in nine counties and never will be.** Nine days of not eating. The word that was struck off her county sheet. A crew that will not sign her shift. A bounty attached to the licence.

**What is still open at the last page of Volume 2.** The claim, standing and unclaimed, now travelling on a county document. Hou Sanliang unpaid and patient. *Kiln-born* in two heads and no paper. The office name Yuan cannot read. The eleven. Ning Wu, alive, on a list, on four registers reading *reconciled*. The Clay Plate against her ribs. Settlement at a little under three-quarters, about six weeks. A crew of nine, ten sleepers, and a crooked licence over the second bunk. A tower lamp burning down the mountain, and a stranger at a desk in the market below it who gives his name to nobody.

## Chapter-length decision (settled once; do not re-record as an exception)

Batch 0007 averaged ~1,300 words and batch 0008 averaged ~811, against the 1,800–2,700 guide in `AGENTS.md`. Both batches carried the same note, "known trade-off, left intact rather than padded," three batches running. **That note is retired.**

Batch 0008 has been repaired to **~1,483 words/chapter, 22,269 words total** (was 12,176). The remedy was scene material, not padding: new scenes and beats inside the existing cards, never new plot. **Standing rule for the rest of the run:** the guide is 1,800–2,700; the floor is 1,300; **a batch that lands a monotone decline across its chapters, or an average under 1,300, is a failed batch.** Do not record a trade-off. Fix the chapters.

**Batch 0009 met the guide on its average without a repair: 28,234 words over fifteen chapters, average ~1,882** (was 28,236 before the volume-close word-level repairs, which cost 2 words net). Ten of the fifteen chapters are individually under 1,800 and are carried by ch 115 (2,657), 116 (2,083) and 118 (2,604); nothing is under the 1,300 floor and the run is not a monotone decline. The material added to reach the average was scene material inside the existing cards — the offering of four coins to the men on the wall, the clerk's apprentice at the sixth column, the good coat at the plank, Feng's three sentences stated in order, Feng writing for an hour on a stool she cannot watch from, the four struck copies and the shelf that was not struck, the no-surety request put in writing at the third hour, Lu Tao's board gone over in the spring, Shen Xiu's eye resting half a count longer on a signature than on a number, the coin put on a shut desk and picked up. **No new plot and no new card beat.** Every paragraph over ninety words in the batch was split before the batch was closed; the longest that remain are exactly ninety, at ch 109, ch 115 and ch 117.

## Canon changes made in batch 0006 (recorded, not made silently)

**Canon change 6 — the day numbering.** `outline/volume-02.md` gives days 1–11 of the ash fall
against chapters, and ch 58 is day 9. Batch 0006 therefore runs **day 10 (ch 59), day 11 (ch 60),
day 12 (ch 61 and 62), day 14 (ch 63), day 15 (ch 64), day 16 (ch 65 and 66), day 17 (ch 67),
day 18 (ch 68, thaw begins), day 19 (ch 69), day 20 (ch 70), day 21 (ch 71, night),
day 22 (ch 72, burial).** The batch ends on **day 22 of the winter**, which is the **fifth day of
the thaw** — *not* the twenty-second day of the thaw. Ch 71's deaths are dated to the twenty-first
day in the burial register entry at ch 72.

**Canon change 7 — the plate clause has a clock.** The sponsorship requires the plate
*within the week of signature* (ch 48 wording, ch 56 schedule). Signed on day 5, that fell due at
**noon on day 12**, which is ch 62. The card did not date it; dating it made ch 62 a scene with
a deadline instead of a scene about a trap, and the clause is now two days overdue and visibly so.
**It is not resolved in batch 0006.** It stays in the sponsors' hands as an unspent weapon for
Volume 3 and later.

**Canon change 8 — the two crew friends carry surnames from ch 59.** They are **Cao Lei** (22)
and **Xu An** (26), surnames only, and they are named on the page in **nine chapters** — 59, 60,
64, 65, 66, 67, 69, 71 and 72. Neither given name is ever spoken; the burial register at ch 72 is
the only document that spells either out in full. The outline's "they stay unnamed" is satisfied in
the sense that matters — **no one ever says their given names aloud, and after ch 72 neither
surname may be used again.**

**Canon change 9 — the quota office.** `outline/volume-02.md` and `bible/characters.md` do not fix
where Shen Xiu's quota office is. Placed at the back of the furnace building with one window and
four tables, with an anteroom of four benches in front of it, and **a frame on its back wall
holding twelve unsigned sheets.** That frame is the one the tithe clerk described at ch 64; she
sees it at ch 69 and enters it in the log in wash code. **Two of the twelve are written in the
roundhouse hand — Captain Lu Tao's hand, off the `Ning Yan. Held.` line on his own barrier board.
That is the real plant and it is recorded in the log, not just on the page.** Who wrote the twelve
sheets is deliberately unexplained for the whole run. The one on hours is held for Volume 7.

**Canon change 10 — the tithe clerk is a minor character with a position.** Three planks, four feet
from the furnace mouth, a brush cut flat, a broken finger set an inch too long, a worse cough than
hers. He counts slowly on purpose because a fast number makes the book unbalanced. He asks her to
say her hours *slowly* so that the number will be right when a clerk reads it in four months
(ch 64), and he writes *member on schedule* against her hours at ch 72. **He is not a villain, not
an ally, and does not recur as a plot device.**

**Canon change 11 — Lowwater is named once as a place a coat came from.** At ch 68 the bounty man
wears a coat that was good in Hanhui. Hanhui is a **place** eleven days down the road, used
correctly. Recorded here because Hanhui has been misused as a firm before.

## The document, as it now stands on the page (end of ch 118)

**Licence to Practise Past Registration.** Clause Four of the Servant Advancement Settlement.
Issued at the **Bureau of Records' county office at Lowwater** by **Registrar Sun He**,
countersigned ch 115 with the season's furnace schedule attached as collateral labour and
**Feng Lushi witnessing**. Three legs: **sponsors' seal (ch 62) · quota clearance (ch 86) ·
county countersignature (ch 115)** — the third leg was walked to: the first filing was refused at
the Hall for want of clearance (ch 70) and the second was refused at Lowwater for want of standing
(ch 88), in the same hour she signed a **pending filing** capped at Condensing 5 with her defect in
her own hand, the sponsors' Foundation writ dead on the same page, and a living surety entered.

**Where it stands now.** Granted. Capped at Condensing stage 5. Barred from Foundation without
relicensing. The defect is entered and the licence restores nothing to the aperture. The **eight
hundred jin standing claim is entered on the back of it as the sixth condition and travels with
it**, which is what the county does when a book is a book, and it is the reason the claim is now
harder to shake than it was in the winter.

**Six conditions, fixed.** (1) Every county hour, in person, in her own hand; no surety again
without relicensing. (2) Hours on the Qinghe furnace schedule, certified, returned at the quarter.
(3) Practice at or below Condensing five. (4) No Foundation without relicensing. (5) The defect
restores nothing. (6) The claim is entered on the licence, travels with it, and is not affected
by it. **Do not re-list these.**

  97, 99, 101) and each carries different weight.

  this batch*, *the volume's only source*, *the volume's loss*. Also out of `chapters/volume-01/
  chapter-0019.md` and `chapter-0024.md`, where the same leak had been standing, plus a `not for+`
  text artifact in ch 24.

  `chapter-0012.md` and `chapter-0055.md`. The book is now at zero for every entry on the `AGENTS.md`
  *Never:* list.

  0008's log additions, its **two** brackets (92, 101), its day numbering, its Settlement clock, and
  the 89–103 titles. This block is what batch 0009 reads first, and it was actively misleading.

**Canon change 18 — the Watch, night 121 (ch 102).** The Watch reaches the tower foot at the ninth
hour, not at the killing. It writes **a body, a road, a ditch; that a man was shouting about a
refused place and the shouting was finished when it arrived; and that one man was carried up the
tower stair alive at the fourth hour with a lamp.** It does not write that the corpse was answering
to an act entered in the layer — **no office in Lowwater has a form with a column for it**, and that
is the mechanism by which the eight hundred jin still stands unanswered at dawn. It records her one
statement, *entered*, as a statement by the injured party and no more than that. Mechanism, no
villain, no speech.

**Canon change 19 — the county holds the burial line and the debt (ch 103).** At the second hour on
day 122 she lodges the burial line at the county office on the square. A clerk asks whether she is
a member of the household; she answers *I am the undersigned*, then *It is what I entered*; he
writes her answer into the corner and stamps it with **the same stamp as the pending filing**. He
then confirms the surety is entered in this county under Du Hong's own name at his own voice, and
she asks him to write the transfer as **Stands to me**, and he writes it **in the county book,
where a county keeps a line**, and says nothing about it. **The county now holds two documents
with her hand on both, neither of them about her advancement, and the debt is on paper before it is
in her head.** She also enters a **fifth** cause at the burial — that the second man would not have
stood where she could reach him had he not been reading his file aloud over a boy in the water —
because a clerk who writes four causes and stops where the story gets comfortable has been softened.

**Canon change 20 — the intake line moves to the ninth hour (ch 95).** The younger man buys a
carrier at the end of the tower lane and learns **her pace**, not her name: *there is a woman sets
the loads down slow.* To break the description she has her tower intake line moved from the fifth
hour to the ninth, **entered by Feng as a tower change and not a request, because a request carries a
reason and a change carries a sheet.** She knows the cost and takes it: the change goes up the same
stair as everything else, and a reader of that stair learns that her hours were moved. Where forty
other hands set loads down, one woman does not.

Also now on the page and carried: **the girl of twenty-four, three times in repair** (ch 91 the four-hour correction in person and the gate man she left in the water; ch 94, where she is asked directly whether she is safe and answers that she is the safest thing in the town and has been since a winter she does not describe; ch 103, where she sends an unasked-for correction slate to a burial); **Feng and the clerks' cover story** — a fall on the stair, carried out to the wash house before the cold got into him — written down by every one of them because it is easier than the other thing; **the counting-room woman** at the third desk, four households read and priced with no hand on her arm (ch 98), which is what moves the pursuit from bodies to households in her own head; **the third desk's sheet goes up at the quarter in the same crate as the rest** (ch 99); **Sun He**, who says the longest sentence he has said to her and it is a clerk telling a holder how to keep a record clean (ch 94).

## Volume 1 acceptance check — `outline/volume-01.md`, fact by fact

1. Body-Forging stage 9 → **Condensing stage 1**, aperture permanently cracked. **DONE** — ch 52.
2. The Ashwood Nine-Step is lost as a technique and held only as habit. **DONE** — ch 51; the source is still untold to anyone but Yuan.
3. Ning Wu's record reads *assessed, reassigned, unreconciled.* **CONFLICT — see canon change 1 below.** The register now reads *reconciled*, bought with a signature in ch 56. The truth is unchanged and is still held by her.
4. Qiu Zhulang is alive, pensioned, and not a villain in waiting. **DONE** — ch 50.
5. Feng Lushi is alive and has copied her name upward. **DONE** — ch 55.
6. Du Hong is an inner disciple because she asked for it. **DONE (unchanged from ch 33) and now paid on the page.** She was still furious with him about the coat in ch 55, and in ch 56 she entered the whole of it in her own hand — the advancement on her recommendation, the report order, the fact that he filed nothing, and the fact that she has known since before the trial and entered nothing until that hour. **He knows she knows, as of ch 56.**
7. Yuan's Settlement has risen twice and fallen once. **DONE (accumulating; running tally now five rises, two falls, past two-thirds)** — ch 54.
8. The Clay Tablet is with Ning Yan and the Elder Hall has only been shown where the Kiln is. **DONE** — ch 56.

Final-chapter image in `outline/volume-01.md`: **honoured exactly** — first snow on the Nine Steps Terrace, the announcement going round and getting the words wrong, Ning Yan on the sixth step with the sponsor's token in her hand which she does not want, asking what it is counting, and not being answered because he is counting.

## Proposed canon changes (recorded explicitly, not made silently)

**Canon change 1 — Ning Wu's register entry.**
`outline/volume-01.md` item 3 and its Concrete Resolution section say his record *remains* *assessed, reassigned, unreconciled*, not answered. The binding batch cards for ch 48 and ch 56 require her to buy the word *reconciled* with the sponsorship signature. The batch cards win. **Canon:** the register of Qinghe and of three other counties reads *reconciled*, entered with the hour, the clerk's name and the fact that it was asked for and not offered. The truth is unchanged: *assessed, reassigned, unreconciled*, reassigned above furnace authority at thirteen, alive, on the Warden's list. The proof of the reassignment is the Clay Plate she is still wearing. The lie stays on her head for Volumes 2–4. `outline/volume-01.md` was **not** edited for this at drafting time — controller-owned planning file — but it **was** edited during the batch-0005 review repair for two unrelated internal contradictions (Clay Tablet, Du Hong). If this canon change is ever reversed, edit the outline bullet with it.

**Canon change 2 — Qiu's line.**
*the shape of a happy life* is reserved by `bible/terminology.md` and `outline/ending.md` for a second speaking by Ning Wu in Volume 16. It had never been spoken on the page. It is now spoken **once**, by Qiu Zhulang, in ch 50. Used once. Do not use it again until Volume 16.

**Canon change 3 — the Winged Office.**
`state/open-threads.md` has carried "the Winged Office: the first thing in the book that reads a System" as a Vol 3 thread, but the phrase had never appeared in the manuscript. Volume 1 is now closed, so it is **not** named in the text. Ch 58 establishes instead: there is a place in the layer where announcements are read after they go out, the office attached to it has a name, and Yuan has never once been able to read it. Volume 2 or 3 may name the office.

**Canon change 4 — the Hall's roof.**
The Assembly Hall of Qinghe loses its roof in ch 52 and is never rebuilt. The open floor is used in Volume 5; the Hall still has no roof in Volume 6. **Confirmed on the page in batch 0006:** it is used for paper only, a clerk says so at ch 61, and water stands in its low corner from ch 61 onward.

**Canon change 5 — the Ashwood Nine-Step source.**
Confirmed still unshared. She refused to explain it on a floor in front of the whole sect (ch 51) and did not tell Yuan either.

**Canon changes 6–11 are recorded in the table above.**

## Volume 2 plan decisions (recorded at planning; canon changes 6–11 now sit on top of them)

1. **The document is named.** The *Licence to Practise Past Registration*, issued at the
   **Bureau of Records' county office at Lowwater**, under **Clause Four of the Servant
   Advancement Settlement**. Three legs: the sponsors' seal, quota clearance from Shen Xiu's
   office, the county countersignature at Lowwater. **Clause Four, not Nine** — ch 41 already
   spent a ninth clause.
2. **Geography.** Lowwater is a county *and* the town under Qinghe Sect. The Bureau's two
   Lowwater premises are different rooms: the copying floor in Feng Lushi's tower, and the
   county office on the square. **There is no Qinghe county office; Qinghe is a sect.**
3. **Hanhui is a place**, eleven days down the road, not a firm. Hou Sanliang is a broker **of
   Lowwater** holding paper on three of the nine struck households.
4. **Every Condensing stage in Volume 2 is announced to the whole bound.** Canon, not a change
   (`bible/power-system.md`, `state/continuity.md` world rule 3). A stage is an ordeal in the
   body and never a silence. **No tribulation** — a tribulation is drawn by a *trial* (ch 52)
   and there is no trial in Volume 2. Each stage therefore ends in a decision about whether the
   sentence is released: ch 78 released, ch 92 released, ch 104 **suppressed at her order**,
   ch 114 **released at her order**.
5. **Yuan takes Ledger exactly once**, at ch 80, on her spoken order, for less than a day, at a
   Settlement spike past nine-tenths. He refuses to hold it again the same day and permanently.
   `outline/series.md` assigns Ledger to Volumes 3–5; this is the one deliberate, recorded
   exception and it is not a band change. **Its price was stated aloud at ch 68 and must not be
   restated at ch 80 — only paid.**
6. **The Merit source of Volume 2 is the kill at ch 100**, and there is no other — Merit settles
   on a listed kill or a cleared trial, and Volume 2 has no trial. At ch 101 he **tells her
   before he spends it** and she says yes: rule three paid in full, the ch 54 wound answered in
   the correct order. The tax takes sequence, not substance, and a left-hand sequence rebuilt
   at ch 78 is gone. Nothing she loses comes back.
7. **Settlement clock, stated once.** Channel is weeks of ordinary use remaining, measured against the
   volume's own rate of ~3.5 chapters a week: ch 58 past two-thirds (~0.67), ~17 weeks → ch 80 past
   nine-tenths (~0.91), ~6½ weeks (the Ledger spike costs ~4½) → ch 100 still ~0.91, **about five
   days left** → ch 101 back to ~0.68, ~11 weeks (Merit spent) → ch 118 ~0.75, ~6 weeks.
   **Without the kill at ch 100 he is dry inside ch 101.** The Merit comes from that one listed kill
   and there is no other source in the volume.
8. **The lie is an exaggeration, not a reversal.** He already told her in ch 57 that a
   designation is a *writ*, not a reward, and does not unsay it. In ch 92–94 he hardens her own
   ch 55 conclusion — that the claim cannot take her quietly — into a floor. She works out alone
   at ch 100 that being Chosen **prices** her rather than sheltering her: the eleven were
   spaced irregularly because they were *spent at different rates*, not found at different
   times. **She does not conclude that the Foundation tax was designed.** That is the Volume 11
   origin answer and is not spent here. Non-forgiveness runs from ch 100 through Volume 3 into
   the opening of Volume 4 — that is the year the milestone requires.
9. **Feng Lushi is not resolved in Volume 2.** He received the four county copies on market day
   (ch 63 — the road opened ch 61), walled them behind the fifth item, the column stays at five, he
   coughs, he witnesses the countersignature at ch 115. **No Bureau audit** (that is the Vol 3 traitor beat)
   and **the offered Lung is neither offered nor refused here** — he takes one in Volume 3.

## Volume 3 Batch 0001 review repair — historical note

Repaired against `logs/next.review.log` without restarting the batch and without changing the
planned plot. **No title, no chapter order, no ending type, no condition, no bracket and no word of
plot changed.** What moved:

- **Paragraph cap, the real defect.** Seventeen paragraphs rendered over ninety words, two over the
  hard hundred-and-twenty ceiling (the three-line wash-code log in **0121** at 164 words and Sun He's
  licence valuation in **0132** at 139). **Every one of the seventeen had the same cause** — two or
  three lines joined by a *single* newline where a blank line belongs, which markdown renders as one
  paragraph. All seventeen are split at their natural beats. **In the ten chapters that took nothing
  but the split, not one non-blank line differs from what was written**, checked line for line against
  the pre-fix file. Longest rendered paragraph is now **90 words** (ch 125 and ch 130). Net across
  the batch: **+18 words.**
- **A garbled merge at the close of 0131.** *"It was the first weeks of summer. in the light of a
  morning"* was two sentences run together with a lowercase start. Now one sentence.
- **A duplicated closing image.** 0119 and 0121 both ended on *the board was still up*. The mat on
  the bank is the better image and the one that carries 0121's actual beat, so **0121 keeps the mat
  and loses the board**; 0119's board-and-chalk close is untouched.
- **A narrator prolepsis at the close of 0132** — *she would not find that out until much later,
  which is the shape of the whole of this* — same family as the banned *little did he know*. Cut.
  The chapter now ends on **one coin and one sheet**, which carries the irony without announcing it.
- **A four-declarative recap at the close of 0126** — *a clerk had sold a copy, a man had read it,
  a room had heard two sentences, a registrar had written an hour* — replaced with a landed beat:
  the dread of being correct, and a woman putting the lamp out and lying down on the good side.
- **`workspace/volume-03-batch-0001/SUMMARY.md` was reporting a check that was false.** It claimed no
  paragraph over ninety words. It now records what is true and states the correction rather than
  quietly replacing it.
  - **Why the check was false, and the rule that follows.** The original number — *longest exactly
    ninety, ch 125 and ch 130* — is precisely the figure the batch has **after** the fix. The check
    was almost certainly run with a splitter that broke on `\n` rather than on a blank line, so it
    measured the file's *lines* and never measured the paragraphs a renderer produces. **A paragraph
    check on a markdown chapter must split on `\n\s*\n`.** Run it that way or it is not a check.

**Day clock, corrected.** Batch 0002's prompt fixed the batch at *day 42 to day 120* and separately at
*stage six about day 81*, which cannot both hold. Reconciled against `outline/volume-03.md`: **stage
six is six weeks of double-schedule labour after day 39, so day 81 at ch 135; stage seven is five
weeks of escort-road work after stage six, so day 116 at ch 148; the two weeks of fever it costs
carry the batch close to day 130.** Batch 0002 therefore runs **day 42–130** with deliberately uneven
pacing — ch 133–135 carry six weeks, the other thirteen carry five — and **Batch 0003 opens at about
day 133.** The day table is in `workspace/volume-03-batch-0002/PROMPT.md`. **This is an arithmetic
correction and moves no beat.**

**Not touched, and flagged for the controller rather than edited:** `reviews/` is empty and
`state/phase-ledger.json` does not exist, so no durable record of this review survives a checkout;
and Batch 0002's prompt still requires the writer to author sixteen chapter cards and sixteen
chapters in one run. Both are controller-owned.

then for six more. The traps are still live in any model that has read the old draft:
a lie that reverses ch 57 · a rule that intra-realm stages are not announced · Ledger retaken in
three chapters · a "Qinghe county office at Lowwater" · Hanhui as a firm · *Cracking* as a furnace
maintenance phase · a second ninth clause · the records room moved out from under the furnace ·
three better rooms where the page says four · the first filing dated twice · the Volume 11 tax
answer handed over at ch 100 · the Bureau's ears re-revealed · Feng audited or his Lung refused
inside Volume 2 · a sealed-effects regulation that cancelled itself · ch 104's suppression
justified by a licence that does not exist until ch 115 · a Settlement table whose rows
contradict the sentence beneath it · Feng receiving the county copies in two chapters · the sworn
non-possession of the plate attributed to the sponsors instead of to **her**.

**All avoided, and three of them answered on the page in batch 0009.** The Bureau's ears were not re-revealed: the slip at ch 110 came from outside the bound with an office Yuan cannot read, and it struck a term rather than answering anything. Ch 104's suppression is **not** justified by a licence that does not exist yet — she loses the paper for that stage on purpose and finds out at ch 115 that a hole is an absence and not a breach. The Settlement table reconciles: **~11 weeks at ch 101 → ~6 weeks at ch 118**, seventeen chapters of ordinary use. **Volume 2 is closed.**
